<a name="section-1"></a>
## SECTION 1 — Design Philosophy

### 1.1 Core Distinction: Recovery ≠ Hedge

These are two fundamentally different systems with different objectives, different risk profiles, and different failure modes. **They must never be conflated, chained implicitly, or assumed to co-activate.**

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        RECOVERY ENGINE                              │
│                                                                     │
│  Objective   →  Improve average entry price                         │
│  Mechanism   →  Add positions in the SAME direction as loss         │
│  Outcome     →  Reduce break-even distance                          │
│  Risk        →  Increases total exposure                            │
│  Exits       →  Coordinate basket exit at break-even or target      │
│                                                                     │
│  "I am losing. I will improve my position to escape faster."        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         HEDGE ENGINE                                │
│                                                                     │
│  Objective   →  Reduce portfolio exposure                           │
│  Mechanism   →  Add positions in the OPPOSITE direction             │
│  Outcome     →  Reduce drawdown and survive adverse movement        │
│  Risk        →  Locks in spread cost and opportunity cost           │
│  Exits       →  Unwind selectively when pressure subsides           │
│                                                                     │
│  "I am losing. I will neutralize exposure to survive this move."    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 1.2 Independence Rules

| Rule | Recovery Engine | Hedge Engine |
|---|---|---|
| May activate without the other | ✅ Yes | ✅ Yes |
| May trigger the other | ❌ Never | ❌ Never |
| Assumes the other is active | ❌ Never | ❌ Never |
| May block the other | ✅ Via Authority Model | ✅ Via Authority Model |
| Shares position sizing | ❌ Independent | ❌ Independent |
| Shares exit logic | ❌ Independent | ❌ Independent |

### 1.3 Strategy Compatibility Matrix

This engine is strategy-agnostic. It integrates with any of the following EA types without modification:

| EA Strategy | Recovery Compatible | Hedge Compatible | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Trend Following | ✅ | ✅ | Recovery follows trend direction |
| HalfTrend | ✅ | ✅ | Recovery on HalfTrend confirmation |
| Breakout | ✅ | ✅ | Hedge on false breakout |
| SMC | ✅ | ✅ | Recovery at BOS/CHoCH levels |
| Mean Reversion | ✅ | ⚠️ Caution | Hedge conflicts with MR philosophy |
| Grid | ✅ | ⚠️ Caution | Grid IS a form of recovery; hedge rarely needed |
| Hybrid | ✅ | ✅ | Requires careful authority configuration |

---

<a name="section-2"></a>
## SECTION 2 — Recovery Engine

### 2.1 Architecture Overview

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        RECOVERY ENGINE                                 │
│                                                                        │
│   [Recovery Trigger Monitor]                                           │
│           │                                                            │
│           ▼                                                            │
│   [Safety Rule Validator] ──── BLOCK ────► [Log & Cooldown]           │
│           │                                                            │
│           ▼ PASS                                                       │
│   [Recovery Mode Selector]                                             │
│     ├── NONE      → Pass-through, no action                            │
│     ├── SOFT      → Fixed distance, fixed lot                          │
│     ├── ADAPTIVE  → ATR-based distance, scaled lot                     │
│     └── CONTROLLED→ Capped steps, multiplier-limited                   │
│           │                                                            │
│           ▼                                                            │
│   [Recovery Order Manager]                                             │
│     ├── Entry: Market or Pending                                       │
│     ├── Lot: BaseLot × RecoveryMultiplier^step                         │
│     └── Comment: [EA_ID][RECOVERY][Step_N]                             │
│           │                                                            │
│           ▼                                                            │
│   [Basket Break-Even Calculator]                                       │
│     └── Recalculate weighted average entry → Emit to Exit Engine       │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 2.2 Recovery Modes

#### MODE: NONE

| Property | Definition |
|---|---|
| **Objective** | Disable recovery entirely. EA operates single-position only. |
| **Risk Profile** | Baseline — no additional exposure added |
| **Intended Use** | Conservative EAs, during optimization, or when capital is constrained |
| **Advantages** | Cleanest risk profile. No compounding exposure. Backtest results are most predictable. Optimizer can properly isolate signal quality. |
| **Failure Modes** | A losing position has no recovery path. Single trade losses directly impact account equity. May cause unnecessary stops on valid setups. |

---

#### MODE: SOFT

| Property | Definition |
|---|---|
| **Objective** | Place a single recovery order at a fixed distance. Simple, predictable. |
| **Risk Profile** | Low-to-Moderate. Maximum 1 additional order per chain. |
| **Intended Use** | Trend Following, HalfTrend, Breakout EAs where brief retracements are common |
| **Advantages** | Minimal complexity. Easy to backtest. Break-even distance cut by ~50% on successful recovery. Spreads cost over 2 orders only. |
| **Failure Modes** | Only one recovery attempt. If price continues beyond recovery entry, exposure doubles with no further mitigation. Does not scale with volatility. |

**SOFT Recovery Flow:**
```
Original Entry (BUY @ 1.3000) ──► Move -50 pts ──► Recovery BUY @ 1.2950
                                                    (Fixed distance = 50 pts)
Basket Break-Even = 1.2975
Exit at 1.2975 + Profit_Buffer = 1.2985
```

---

#### MODE: ADAPTIVE

| Property | Definition |
|---|---|
| **Objective** | Place recovery orders at dynamic distances based on market volatility (ATR). Scale lot size proportionally to maintain consistent break-even distance. |
| **Risk Profile** | Moderate. Exposure scales with step count but remains volatility-calibrated. |
| **Intended Use** | SMC, HalfTrend, Hybrid EAs operating across variable-volatility instruments or timeframes |
| **Advantages** | Self-adjusting to market conditions. Avoids premature recovery in high-volatility environments. Break-even always proportionally achievable. Works well in trending markets. |
| **Failure Modes** | ATR can expand dramatically in news events — recovery distance can become unreachable. Complexity makes manual intervention harder. Optimization surface is larger. |

**ADAPTIVE Recovery Calculation:**
```
Recovery Distance = ATR(Period) × ATR_Multiplier
Recovery Lot     = BaseLot × RecoveryMultiplier^step
Break-Even       = Σ(Lot_i × Entry_i) / Σ(Lot_i)
```

---

#### MODE: CONTROLLED

| Property | Definition |
|---|---|
| **Objective** | Full recovery sequence with hard caps on steps and multiplier ceiling. Structured martingale with enforced limits. |
| **Risk Profile** | Moderate-to-High. Maximum exposure is deterministic and pre-calculated. |
| **Intended Use** | Grid EAs, Mean Reversion EAs, Hybrid systems where recovery sequences are core to the strategy |
| **Advantages** | Maximum exposure is known before trade entry. Break-even analysis is deterministic. Safety caps prevent runaway exposure. Suitable for asset classes with mean-reversion characteristics. |
| **Failure Modes** | Maximum steps can still produce significant drawdown if market trends strongly. Must not be used on trending assets without a stop condition. A full chain failure produces the largest single loss of all modes. |

**CONTROLLED Recovery Pre-Calculation:**
```
Step | Lot Multiplier | Cumulative Lots | Approx. Break-Even Shift
-----|----------------|-----------------|-------------------------
  0  | 1.0×           | 1.0             | Baseline (original entry)
  1  | 1.5×           | 2.5             | -60% of original distance
  2  | 2.0×           | 4.5             | -78% of original distance
  3  | 2.5×           | 7.0             | -86% of original distance
MAX  | Capped         | Capped          | Hard stop beyond this
```

### 2.3 Mode Selection Decision Tree

```
Is recovery needed?
  │
  ├─► NO  → MODE: NONE
  │
  └─► YES
        │
        ├─► Single retracement expected, trend intact?
        │     └─► MODE: SOFT
        │
        ├─► Variable volatility, dynamic conditions?
        │     └─► MODE: ADAPTIVE
        │
        └─► Mean reversion / grid / known range expected?
              └─► MODE: CONTROLLED
```

---

<a name="section-3"></a>
## SECTION 3 — Recovery Triggers

### 3.1 Trigger Architecture

Each trigger type is a **module**. EAs enable exactly the triggers relevant to their signal logic. Multiple triggers can be combined with AND/OR logic.

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                  TRIGGER EVALUATION ENGINE                   │
│                                                              │
│  [Distance]  [Candle]  [Trend]  [Structure]  [DD]  [Volatility]│
│       │          │        │          │         │        │    │
│       └──────────┴────────┴──────────┴─────────┴────────┘    │
│                           │                                  │
│                    [Logic Combiner]                          │
│                    AND / OR / WEIGHTED                       │
│                           │                                  │
│              ┌────────────┴───────────────┐                  │
│              │                            │                  │
│        TRIGGER FIRED                TRIGGER BLOCKED          │
│              │                            │                  │
│        [Safety Validator]          [Log Rejection]           │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### 3.2 Trigger Type 1: Distance Recovery

**Concept:** Recovery is triggered when price moves a defined distance against the open position.

| Variant | Formula | Default |
|---|---|---|
| Fixed Points | `CurrentPrice - EntryPrice > FixedPoints` | 50 pts |
| ATR-Based | `CurrentPrice - EntryPrice > ATR(14)` | 1× ATR(14) |
| ATR Multiplier | `CurrentPrice - EntryPrice > ATR(14) × Multiplier` | 1.5× ATR(14) |

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Strengths** | Simple, deterministic, easy to backtest. No indicator dependency. Fires reliably. |
| **Weaknesses** | Ignores market structure. May fire during normal retracements of a valid trend. Fixed distance becomes irrelevant across instruments with different volatility profiles. |
| **Best Use Cases** | SOFT mode recovery on Trend Following and HalfTrend EAs. Grid EAs where fixed levels are part of the strategy design. Low-volatility instruments (JPY pairs, stable indices). |

---

### 3.3 Trigger Type 2: Candle Recovery

**Concept:** Recovery is triggered by a specific candlestick formation suggesting local momentum reversal or exhaustion.

| Variant | Condition |
|---|---|
| Engulfing | Prior candle body fully engulfed by current candle in recovery direction |
| Pin Bar | Shadow ≥ 2× body length, close ≥ 60% of range in recovery direction |
| Strong Close | Close is in top/bottom 25% of candle range AND candle range ≥ 1.0× ATR(14) |

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Strengths** | Adds confluence to recovery decision. Reduces recovery entries at arbitrary distances. Aligns with price action logic already present in many signal engines. |
| **Weaknesses** | Prone to false signals in consolidating markets. Requires sufficient candle formation — may delay recovery beyond optimal entry point. Sensitive to broker feed quality. |
| **Best Use Cases** | SMC EAs, HalfTrend EAs. ADAPTIVE mode recovery where candle confirmation provides additional confidence. Higher timeframe (H1, H4) recovery decisions. |

---

### 3.4 Trigger Type 3: Trend Recovery

**Concept:** Recovery is triggered only when the trend condition that generated the original signal is still intact.

| Variant | Condition |
|---|---|
| HalfTrend Valid | HalfTrend indicator still points in original trade direction |
| EMA Alignment | EMA(fast) still above/below EMA(slow) in trade direction |
| Trend Score | Internal trend score ≥ defined threshold (e.g. ≥ 60/100) |

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Strengths** | Highest quality recovery entries. Ensures recovery only occurs in a directionally valid context. Prevents recovery into a full trend reversal. |
| **Weaknesses** | If trend invalidates before recovery threshold, recovery is blocked — position may be left unmanaged. Lagging indicators may confirm trend validity too late. |
| **Best Use Cases** | Trend Following EAs, HalfTrend EAs. ADAPTIVE and CONTROLLED modes where recovery quality matters. Medium-to-long timeframes (H4, D1). |

---

### 3.5 Trigger Type 4: Structure Recovery

**Concept:** Recovery is triggered at key market structure levels — Break of Structure (BOS), Change of Character (CHoCH), or swing points.

| Variant | Condition |
|---|---|
| BOS | Price breaks the most recent swing high/low in recovery direction |
| CHoCH | HH→HL or LH→LL pattern shift detected in recovery direction |
| Higher Low (HL) | For BUY recovery: a confirmed Higher Low on chart |
| Lower High (LH) | For SELL recovery: a confirmed Lower High on chart |

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Strengths** | Institutionally validated entry levels. Recovery aligns with Smart Money logic. Provides high-probability entry context. |
| **Weaknesses** | Structure breaks can be false — stop hunts, liquidity grabs. Structure identification requires additional computation. Fewer triggers in low-structure environments. |
| **Best Use Cases** | SMC EAs, Breakout EAs. ADAPTIVE mode recovery where structure confluence is essential. H1–H4 timeframes. |

---

### 3.6 Trigger Type 5: DD Recovery

**Concept:** Recovery is triggered based on the floating drawdown level of the open position or the full basket.

| Variant | Condition |
|---|---|
| Floating DD % | `FloatingLoss / AccountBalance > Threshold_%` |
| Cycle DD % | `CycleLoss / CycleStartBalance > Threshold_%` |

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Strengths** | Directly tied to account impact. Prevents recovery from triggering on minor price noise. Ensures recovery is proportional to actual financial stress. |
| **Weaknesses** | Delayed trigger relative to price-based methods. In fast-moving markets, DD may exceed recovery threshold before recovery can execute. Can create urgency-based decisions that override quality. |
| **Best Use Cases** | CONTROLLED mode recovery. Grid and Mean Reversion EAs where DD thresholds are pre-defined as strategy parameters. Risk-first frameworks where DD is the primary monitoring variable. |

---

### 3.7 Trigger Type 6: Volatility Recovery

**Concept:** Recovery is triggered based on ATR expansion or contraction relative to a reference period.

| Variant | Condition |
|---|---|
| ATR Expansion | `ATR(current) > ATR(reference) × Expansion_Multiplier` |
| ATR Contraction | `ATR(current) < ATR(reference) × Contraction_Multiplier` |

| Attribute | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Strengths** | ATR expansion: recovery during high-momentum moves where trend continuation is likely. ATR contraction: recovery during consolidation where mean reversion is most predictable. |
| **Weaknesses** | ATR is a lagging indicator. Expansion can signal a trend already exhausted. Contraction signals can be followed by further contraction — no reversal guaranteed. |
| **Best Use Cases** | ATR Expansion → Breakout EAs, Trend Following EAs. ATR Contraction → Mean Reversion EAs, Grid EAs. Both → Hybrid systems adapting to regime. |

---

### 3.8 Trigger Comparison Matrix

| Trigger Type | Speed | Noise Resistance | Structure Awareness | Best Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Distance | Fast | Low | None | SOFT, CONTROLLED |
| Candle | Medium | Medium | Medium | ADAPTIVE |
| Trend | Medium | High | Low | ADAPTIVE |
| Structure | Slow | High | High | ADAPTIVE |
| DD | Slow | High | None | CONTROLLED |
| Volatility | Medium | Medium | None | ADAPTIVE, CONTROLLED |

---

<a name="section-4"></a>
## SECTION 4 — Recovery Safety Rules

### 4.1 Safety Rule Registry

All safety rules are evaluated **before** any recovery order is submitted. A single rule violation blocks the entire recovery action.

```
Recovery Request Received
         │
         ▼
 ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │                  SAFETY RULE GAUNTLET                         │
 │                                                               │
 │  Rule 1: MaxRecoverySteps         ──► BLOCK if exceeded       │
 │  Rule 2: RecoveryMultiplier       ──► BLOCK if > cap          │
 │  Rule 3: RecoveryDistance         ──► BLOCK if < minimum      │
 │  Rule 4: RecoveryCooldown         ──► BLOCK if in cooldown     │
 │  Rule 5: OneRecoveryChainAtATime  ──► BLOCK if chain active   │
 │  Rule 6: BlockRecoveryAfterLoss   ──► BLOCK if loss count hit │
 │  Rule 7: StopRecoveryAtDD         ──► BLOCK if DD exceeded    │
 │  Rule 8: CloseAllAtDD             ──► EMERGENCY FLATTEN       │
 │                                                               │
 │  ALL PASS ──► Recovery Order Submitted                        │
 └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### 4.2 Rule Definitions and Defaults

#### RULE 1: MaxRecoverySteps

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `MaxRecoverySteps` |
| **Type** | Integer |
| **Default** | `3` |
| **Recommended Range** | 1 – 5 |
| **Function** | Hard ceiling on the number of recovery orders in a single chain. Step 0 = original entry. Step 1–N = recovery entries. |
| **Behavior on Violation** | Recovery blocked. Position held. Basket managed by Exit Engine only. |
| **Warning** | Setting > 5 produces exponential lot growth. Never exceed unless strategy is explicitly designed for it and capital is fully allocated. |

---

#### RULE 2: RecoveryMultiplier

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `RecoveryMultiplier` |
| **Type** | Float |
| **Default** | `1.5` |
| **Recommended Range** | 1.0 – 2.0 |
| **Function** | Lot size multiplier at each recovery step. `RecoveryLot_N = BaseLot × RecoveryMultiplier^N` |
| **Behavior on Violation** | If computed lot would exceed `MaxLotSize`, recovery is blocked. |
| **Warning** | Multiplier of 2.0 over 3 steps = 8× base lot. Always pre-calculate maximum exposure before deployment. |

**Pre-Calculation Table (BaseLot = 0.01, MaxLotSize = 0.10):**

| Step | Multiplier 1.0× | Multiplier 1.5× | Multiplier 2.0× |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 (original) | 0.01 | 0.01 | 0.01 |
| 1 | 0.01 | 0.015 | 0.02 |
| 2 | 0.01 | 0.023 | 0.04 |
| 3 | 0.01 | 0.034 | **0.08** |
| 4 | 0.01 | 0.051 | **0.16 → BLOCKED** |

---

#### RULE 3: RecoveryDistance

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `RecoveryDistance_Min` (points) |
| **Type** | Integer |
| **Default** | `20` pts (5-digit brokers) |
| **Recommended Range** | 10 – 100 pts depending on instrument |
| **Function** | Minimum distance between existing recovery entry and proposed new recovery entry. Prevents stacking orders too close together. |
| **Behavior on Violation** | Recovery order deferred until price reaches minimum separation. |
| **Warning** | Too small → excessive order density, high spread cost. Too large → recovery unreachable in time. Calibrate per instrument ATR. |

---

#### RULE 4: RecoveryCooldown

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `RecoveryCooldown_Seconds` |
| **Type** | Integer |
| **Default** | `300` seconds (5 minutes) |
| **Recommended Range** | 60 – 1800 seconds |
| **Function** | Minimum elapsed time between consecutive recovery entries within the same chain. |
| **Behavior on Violation** | Recovery request queued. Evaluated again after cooldown expires. |
| **Rationale** | Prevents multiple recovery orders being stacked during rapid price movement (e.g., news spike). |

---

#### RULE 5: OneRecoveryChainAtATime

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `OneRecoveryChainAtATime` |
| **Type** | Boolean |
| **Default** | `true` |
| **Function** | If a recovery chain is already active on Symbol A, no new recovery chain may be started on any other position until Symbol A's chain is resolved. |
| **Behavior when Active** | New recovery requests from other positions are queued or blocked. |
| **Rationale** | Prevents compounding exposure across multiple simultaneous recovery chains. Portfolio-level risk control. |

---

#### RULE 6: BlockRecoveryAfterLoss

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count` |
| **Type** | Integer |
| **Default** | `2` |
| **Function** | Block recovery for `BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown` seconds after N consecutive chain losses. |
| **Parameter (Cooldown)** | `BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds` = `3600` (1 hour) |
| **Behavior** | Recovery module enters "cooling" state. All recovery requests rejected until timer expires. |
| **Rationale** | Consecutive chain losses indicate regime incompatibility. Prevents continued exposure amplification into a losing market condition. |

---

#### RULE 7: StopRecoveryAtDD

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct` |
| **Type** | Float (percentage) |
| **Default** | `15.0` (%) |
| **Function** | If account floating DD exceeds threshold, all recovery activities are suspended immediately. No new recovery orders may be placed. |
| **Behavior** | Recovery module freezes. Basket held. Exit Engine takes full control. Hedge Engine may activate (per its own authority). |
| **Rationale** | At 15% DD, the priority is capital preservation — not break-even recovery. |

---

#### RULE 8: CloseAllAtDD

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `CloseAllAtDD_Pct` |
| **Type** | Float (percentage) |
| **Default** | `25.0` (%) |
| **Function** | If account floating DD exceeds this hard threshold, ALL positions are closed immediately — including any active recovery chain. |
| **Behavior** | Emergency flatten. Overrides all engines. Logged as emergency event. EA enters `HALTED` state until manual reset. |
| **Rationale** | This is the last line of defense. It ensures the account is never at risk of margin call due to unmanaged recovery exposure. |

---

### 4.3 Default Configuration Summary

```yaml
recovery_safety:
  MaxRecoverySteps: 3
  RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5
  RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 20
  RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300
  OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true
  BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2
  BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600
  StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 15.0
  CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 25.0
```

---

<a name="section-5"></a>
## SECTION 5 — Hedge Engine

### 5.1 Architecture Overview

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                          HEDGE ENGINE                                │
│                                                                      │
│   [Hedge Trigger Monitor]                                            │
│           │                                                          │
│           ▼                                                          │
│   [Safety Rule Validator] ──── BLOCK ────► [Log & Cooldown]         │
│           │                                                          │
│           ▼ PASS                                                     │
│   [Hedge Mode Selector]                                              │
│     ├── OFF        → No hedge active                                 │
│     ├── EMERGENCY  → Full or near-full hedge, immediate              │
│     ├── PARTIAL    → Defined ratio hedge                             │
│     ├── BREAKOUT   → Counter-directional hedge on breakout           │
│     ├── TREND      → Hedge against detected trend reversal           │
│     └── RECOVERY   → Hedge while recovery chain is active            │
│           │                                                          │
│           ▼                                                          │
│   [Hedge Order Manager]                                              │
│     ├── HedgeLot = ExposureLot × HedgeRatio                         │
│     ├── Direction: opposite of net basket direction                  │
│     └── Comment: [EA_ID][HEDGE][Mode][Layer_N]                       │
│           │                                                          │
│           ▼                                                          │
│   [Hedge Unwind Monitor]                                             │
│     └── HedgeTimeout / Condition Cleared → Emit unwind to Exit Engine│
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 5.2 Hedge Modes

#### MODE: OFF

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Description** | Hedge engine is entirely inactive. No hedge positions are opened under any condition. |
| **When to Use** | Conservative strategies, single-currency EAs, or when broker conditions do not support hedging. |
| **Effect** | All hedge triggers are ignored. `HedgeEnabled=false` enforces this mode regardless of other settings. |

---

#### MODE: EMERGENCY

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Description** | Activated when account DD exceeds `EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct`. Places a hedge equal to or near the full net open exposure immediately, without waiting for signal confirmation. |
| **Trigger** | DD-based. Automatic. Highest priority within Hedge Engine. |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 1.0 (100% of net exposure) — full neutralization |
| **Exit Condition** | Price recovery to `HedgeBreakEven_Buffer` OR `HedgeTimeout` expires |
| **Risk** | Locks in current loss level. Spread cost on unwind. Opportunity cost if market reverses favorably. |
| **Rationale** | Survival. Stop the bleeding. Evaluate after hedge is placed. |

---

#### MODE: PARTIAL

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Description** | Activated by moderate DD or exposure threshold. Places a partial hedge covering a defined percentage of net exposure. |
| **Trigger** | Exposure threshold or DD threshold (lower than Emergency) |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 0.3 – 0.7 (configurable) — partial neutralization |
| **Exit Condition** | Exposure normalizes OR condition clears |
| **Risk** | Does not fully protect — adverse movement beyond hedge ratio still produces losses. |
| **Rationale** | Reduce delta without committing to full neutralization. Retain upside potential. |

---

#### MODE: BREAKOUT

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Description** | Hedge is placed in the direction of a confirmed breakout, counter to the original trade direction. Designed to profit from or survive a false breakout that has now confirmed as genuine. |
| **Trigger** | BOS confirmation, range breakout, or momentum threshold |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 0.5 – 1.0 (configurable based on breakout strength) |
| **Exit Condition** | Breakout fails and reverts OR target reached on hedge side |
| **Risk** | If breakout reverses, hedge generates a loss added to original position loss. Double jeopardy. |
| **Rationale** | When a breakout is confirmed against the position, a hedge allows survival and potential profit on the new direction while managing original exposure. |

---

#### MODE: TREND

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Description** | Hedge is placed when trend analysis indicates a confirmed reversal against the open position. The hedge captures the new trend direction. |
| **Trigger** | Trend reversal signal: EMA crossover, HalfTrend flip, trend score collapse |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 0.5 – 1.0 |
| **Exit Condition** | Trend returns to original direction OR hedge profit target reached |
| **Risk** | If trend indicator gives a false reversal signal, both the original position and the hedge are simultaneously losing. |
| **Rationale** | When trend evidence is strong, the hedge reduces original exposure while participating in the new direction. |

---

#### MODE: RECOVERY

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Description** | A partial or full hedge is placed specifically while a recovery chain is active. The hedge neutralizes additional risk added by recovery orders while the chain resolves toward break-even. |
| **Trigger** | Recovery chain active AND position DD > `RecoveryHedgeDD_Pct` |
| **Hedge Ratio** | Calculated as: `HedgeLot = RecoveryExtraExposure × HedgeRatio_Recovery` |
| **Exit Condition** | Recovery chain basket reaches break-even target. Hedge unwound simultaneously with basket exit. |
| **Risk** | Recovery hedge reduces break-even improvement — the hedge's opposite direction partially offsets gains from recovery entries. Must be used cautiously. |
| **Rationale** | Provides a safety ceiling on recovery chain exposure. The recovery still proceeds but maximum loss is bounded. |

---

<a name="section-6"></a>
## SECTION 6 — Hedge Triggers

### 6.1 Trigger Definitions

#### TRIGGER 1: Emergency DD Hedge

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Trigger Condition** | `FloatingDD_Pct >= EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct` (default: 15%) |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 1.0 (100% — full neutralization) |
| **Expected Behavior** | Immediate market order opposite to net basket direction. All other engines notified. Recovery engine suspended. |
| **Risk** | Position locked. Spread cost on both sides. If market reverses immediately, opportunity is missed. Cannot profit on original position until hedge is unwound. |

---

#### TRIGGER 2: Partial Exposure Hedge

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Trigger Condition** | `NetExposureLots >= MaxExposureLots_Threshold` OR `FloatingDD_Pct >= PartialHedgeDD_Pct` (default: 8%) |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 0.3 – 0.5 (configurable) |
| **Expected Behavior** | Partial hedge placed. Original position continues. Net delta reduced but not eliminated. |
| **Risk** | Insufficient protection if adverse movement accelerates. May require escalation to Emergency mode. |

---

#### TRIGGER 3: Breakout Hedge

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Trigger Condition** | Price breaks confirmed structure level (BOS) against original trade direction with momentum confirmation (`Momentum_Candle = true OR ATR_Expansion = true`) |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 0.5 – 1.0 (proportional to breakout strength) |
| **Expected Behavior** | Hedge placed in breakout direction. Both positions monitored. If original trade's break-even is reached, original closed. Hedge held independently. |
| **Risk** | Breakout may be a stop hunt — hedge position immediately goes underwater. Spread costs on both sides. |

---

#### TRIGGER 4: Trend Reversal Hedge

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Trigger Condition** | Primary trend indicator signals reversal: `EMA_Cross_Confirmed AND HalfTrend_Flip AND TrendScore < 40` |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 0.5 – 0.8 |
| **Expected Behavior** | Hedge placed in new trend direction. Original position held pending signal recovery OR closed at next break-even opportunity. |
| **Risk** | Multiple indicator confirmation still produces false reversals in choppy markets. Hedge adds cost. |

---

#### TRIGGER 5: Candle Hedge

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Trigger Condition** | Strong reversal candle formation against position: Bearish Engulfing (for BUY position), Bullish Engulfing (for SELL position) with close in top/bottom 20% of range AND size ≥ 1.5× ATR(14) |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 0.3 – 0.5 |
| **Expected Behavior** | Quick partial hedge placed at candle close. Re-evaluated on the next candle. If reversal continues → escalate to Trend Reversal Hedge. If reversal fails → unwind. |
| **Risk** | Single-candle signals have high false-positive rates, especially on lower timeframes. |

---

#### TRIGGER 6: Structure Hedge

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Trigger Condition** | Key SMC structure breached against original trade: CHoCH confirmed + liquidity pool swept on the opposite side |
| **Hedge Ratio** | 0.5 – 1.0 |
| **Expected Behavior** | Hedge placed at CHoCH confirmation. Treated as high-conviction structural change. Held until original position can be exited at break-even or defined loss. |
| **Risk** | CHoCH misidentification in complex market conditions. Requires clean price action environment. |

---

#### TRIGGER 7: Portfolio Hedge

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Trigger Condition** | Portfolio Guardian signals: `PortfolioDD_Pct >= PortfolioHedgeThreshold_Pct` OR `CorrelatedExposure_Pct >= CorrelationThreshold_Pct` |
| **Hedge Ratio** | Calculated at portfolio level — proportional to correlated exposure |
| **Expected Behavior** | Hedge placed on most liquid correlated instrument (or index if available). Reduces systemic exposure across correlated positions. |
| **Risk** | Correlation assumptions can break down during risk-off events — the very moment portfolio hedges are most needed. |

---

### 6.2 Hedge Trigger Comparison Matrix

| Trigger | Speed | Signal Quality | Hedge Ratio | Best Mode |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Emergency DD | Immediate | N/A (DD-driven) | 1.0 | EMERGENCY |
| Partial Exposure | Medium | High | 0.3–0.5 | PARTIAL |
| Breakout | Medium | Medium | 0.5–1.0 | BREAKOUT |
| Trend Reversal | Slow | High | 0.5–0.8 | TREND |
| Candle | Fast | Low–Medium | 0.3–0.5 | PARTIAL |
| Structure | Slow | High | 0.5–1.0 | TREND, RECOVERY |
| Portfolio | Medium | High | Variable | PARTIAL, EMERGENCY |

---

<a name="section-7"></a>
## SECTION 7 — Hedge Safety Rules

### 7.1 Safety Rule Registry

All hedge safety rules are evaluated before any hedge order is placed. A single violation blocks the hedge action and logs the rejection.

```
Hedge Request Received
         │
         ▼
 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │                  HEDGE SAFETY GAUNTLET                           │
 │                                                                  │
 │  Rule 1: MaxHedgeLayers     ──► BLOCK if exceeded                │
 │  Rule 2: MaxHedgeExposure   ──► BLOCK if net exposure too high   │
 │  Rule 3: MaxHedgeRatio      ──► BLOCK if ratio > cap             │
 │  Rule 4: HedgeCooldown      ──► BLOCK if in cooldown             │
 │  Rule 5: HedgeTimeout       ──► AUTO-UNWIND if timeout exceeded  │
 │  Rule 6: EmergencyFlatten   ──► FLATTEN ALL if threshold hit     │
 │  Rule 7: NoRecursiveHedge   ──► BLOCK hedge-on-hedge             │
 │                                                                  │
 │  ALL PASS ──► Hedge Order Submitted                              │
 └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### 7.2 Rule Definitions and Defaults

#### RULE 1: MaxHedgeLayers

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `MaxHedgeLayers` |
| **Type** | Integer |
| **Default** | `2` |
| **Recommended Range** | 1 – 3 |
| **Function** | Maximum number of simultaneous hedge positions open at any one time (across all modes). |
| **Behavior on Violation** | New hedge request blocked. Existing hedge positions managed by Exit Engine. |
| **Rationale** | Multiple hedge layers create complex net exposure that is difficult to manage and unwind cleanly. |

---

#### RULE 2: MaxHedgeExposure

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `MaxHedgeExposure_Lots` |
| **Type** | Float |
| **Default** | `BaseLot × 5.0` |
| **Function** | Maximum total lot size across all active hedge positions. |
| **Behavior on Violation** | New hedge order lot is reduced to fit within remaining capacity, or rejected if no capacity remains. |
| **Rationale** | Prevents hedge positions from themselves becoming a source of excessive exposure. |

---

#### RULE 3: MaxHedgeRatio

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `MaxHedgeRatio` |
| **Type** | Float |
| **Default** | `1.0` (100% of original net exposure) |
| **Recommended Range** | 0.3 – 1.0 |
| **Function** | No hedge position may exceed this fraction of the current net original position exposure. A ratio of 1.0 allows full neutralization; 0.5 allows half-neutralization maximum. |
| **Behavior on Violation** | Hedge lot capped at `MaxHedgeRatio × NetExposureLots`. |
| **Rationale** | Prevents over-hedging — a situation where the hedge creates more directional exposure than the original position. |

---

#### RULE 4: HedgeCooldown

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `HedgeCooldown_Seconds` |
| **Type** | Integer |
| **Default** | `600` seconds (10 minutes) |
| **Recommended Range** | 300 – 3600 seconds |
| **Function** | Minimum elapsed time between consecutive hedge orders. Applies per symbol. |
| **Behavior on Violation** | Hedge request queued. Evaluated after cooldown expires. |
| **Rationale** | Prevents stacking hedge layers in rapid succession during high-volatility events. |

---

#### RULE 5: HedgeTimeout

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `HedgeTimeout_Seconds` |
| **Type** | Integer |
| **Default** | `14400` seconds (4 hours) |
| **Recommended Range** | 3600 – 86400 seconds |
| **Function** | If a hedge position has been open for longer than `HedgeTimeout_Seconds` and has not been unwound by normal conditions, it is automatically closed at market. |
| **Behavior on Trigger** | Forced unwind at market. Logged as `HEDGE_TIMEOUT_UNWIND`. |
| **Rationale** | Hedges are temporary risk management tools — they are not permanent positions. A hedge left open indefinitely accumulates swap cost and locks capital. |

---

#### RULE 6: EmergencyFlatten

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct` |
| **Type** | Float (percentage) |
| **Default** | `30.0` (%) |
| **Function** | If combined account DD from all positions (including active hedges) reaches this threshold, ALL positions — original and hedge — are closed immediately. |
| **Behavior on Trigger** | Emergency market close all. EA enters `HALTED` state. Manual review required before restart. |
| **Rationale** | Ensures that a failed hedge does not compound the original drawdown beyond survival. The hedge itself can become a liability — this rule closes everything. |

---

#### RULE 7: NoRecursiveHedge

| Property | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Parameter** | `NoRecursiveHedge` |
| **Type** | Boolean (enforced) |
| **Default** | `true` (always enforced) |
| **Function** | A hedge position is tagged as `[HEDGE]`. The Hedge Engine is prohibited from placing a hedge on a position already tagged as `[HEDGE]`. |
| **Behavior** | Any attempt to hedge a hedge is blocked at order submission. Logged as `RECURSIVE_HEDGE_BLOCKED`. |
| **Rationale** | Recursive hedging creates circular exposure that grows without bound and cannot be unwound without incurring a defined loss. This rule is non-negotiable. |

---

### 7.3 Default Configuration Summary

```yaml
hedge_safety:
  MaxHedgeLayers: 2
  MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 5.0   # BaseLot × 5.0
  MaxHedgeRatio: 1.0
  HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600
  HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 14400
  EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 30.0
  NoRecursiveHedge: true                 # Enforced. Not configurable.
```

---

<a name="section-8"></a>
## SECTION 8 — Authority Model

### 8.1 Authority Hierarchy

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                    AUTHORITY HIERARCHY                       ║
║                                                              ║
║   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   ║
║   │  TIER 1 — RISK ENGINE                                │   ║
║   │  "Account survival is the only non-negotiable rule." │   ║
║   │  ► Sets CloseAllAtDD_Pct / EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct   │   ║
║   │  ► May override ANY engine at any time               │   ║
║   │  ► No engine may override Risk Engine                │   ║
║   └──────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘   ║
║                              │  governs                      ║
║   ┌──────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────┐   ║
║   │  TIER 2 — HEDGE ENGINE                               │   ║
║   │  "Reduce exposure before it becomes a crisis."       │   ║
║   │  ► May suspend Recovery Engine                       │   ║
║   │  ► May reduce Signal Engine's new trade permission   │   ║
║   │  ► Cannot override Risk Engine                       │   ║
║   └──────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘   ║
║                              │  governs                      ║
║   ┌──────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────┐   ║
║   │  TIER 3 — RECOVERY ENGINE                            │   ║
║   │  "Improve position to escape loss efficiently."      │   ║
║   │  ► May queue Signal Engine entries                   │   ║
║   │  ► Cannot override Hedge or Risk Engine              │   ║
║   │  ► Suspended when Hedge Engine is in EMERGENCY mode  │   ║
║   └──────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘   ║
║                              │  governs                      ║
║   ┌──────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────┐   ║
║   │  TIER 4 — SIGNAL ENGINE                              │   ║
║   │  "Generate directional trade opportunities."         │   ║
║   │  ► New trades blocked during Recovery chain          │   ║
║   │  ► New trades blocked during EMERGENCY hedge         │   ║
║   │  ► Provides trigger context to Recovery and Hedge    │   ║
║   └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
```

### 8.2 Authority Rationale

| Rule | Reasoning |
|---|---|
| **Risk Engine ranks first** | No strategy objective justifies account destruction. A signal may be wrong; risk limits are always right. |
| **Hedge ranks above Recovery** | Recovery increases exposure. If the hedge engine determines that exposure must be reduced, adding recovery orders directly contradicts that objective. Hedge authority over Recovery prevents conflicting actions. |
| **Recovery ranks above Signal** | A recovery chain changes the account's position structure. New signals entering while a recovery chain is active would create orphaned positions that interfere with basket management and break-even calculation. |
| **Signal ranks last** | The Signal Engine's job is to find opportunities. All other engines exist to manage what happens after signals are acted upon. Signals must respect the current account state. |

### 8.3 Cross-Engine Communication Protocol

| Event | Emitter | Receiver | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DD_THRESHOLD_BREACH` | Risk Engine | All Engines | Engines evaluate their suspension conditions |
| `EMERGENCY_FLATTEN` | Risk Engine | All Engines | All positions closed. All queues cleared. |
| `HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE` | Hedge Engine | Recovery Engine | Recovery suspended until HEDGE_RESOLVED |
| `HEDGE_RESOLVED` | Hedge Engine | Recovery Engine | Recovery engine may resume evaluation |
| `RECOVERY_CHAIN_ACTIVE` | Recovery Engine | Signal Engine | New trade entries blocked |
| `RECOVERY_CHAIN_CLOSED` | Recovery Engine | Signal Engine | New trade entries permitted |
| `RECOVERY_BREAK_EVEN_HIT` | Recovery Engine | Exit Engine | Close basket at market |

---

<a name="section-9"></a>
## SECTION 9 — Feature Gating

### 9.1 Design Principle

Feature gating ensures that **disabled modules have zero runtime cost, zero side effects, and are completely invisible to the optimizer**. An optimizer running with `RecoveryEnabled=false` must not consider any recovery parameters in its parameter space — as if those parameters do not exist.

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                      FEATURE GATE EVALUATION                       │
│                   (runs before any engine logic)                   │
│                                                                    │
│  RecoveryEnabled = false  ──► Skip ALL recovery evaluation         │
│                                Recovery parameters: IGNORED        │
│                                Recovery triggers: DISABLED         │
│                                Recovery orders: BLOCKED            │
│                                                                    │
│  HedgeEnabled = false     ──► Skip ALL hedge evaluation            │
│                                Hedge parameters: IGNORED           │
│                                Hedge triggers: DISABLED            │
│                                Hedge orders: BLOCKED               │
│                                                                    │
│  Both = false             ──► Engine runs signal-only              │
│                                Cleanest backtest baseline           │
│                                                                    │
│  Both = true              ──► Full engine active                   │
│                                Authority model enforced            │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 9.2 Gate Registry

| Gate Parameter | Type | Default | Effect When False |
|---|---|---|---|
| `RecoveryEnabled` | Boolean | `false` | All recovery parameters, triggers, and order logic disabled. Recovery module does not execute. |
| `HedgeEnabled` | Boolean | `false` | All hedge parameters, triggers, and order logic disabled. Hedge module does not execute. |
| `RecoveryMode` | Enum | `NONE` | When `NONE`, same effect as `RecoveryEnabled=false` with logging |
| `HedgeMode` | Enum | `OFF` | When `OFF`, same effect as `HedgeEnabled=false` with logging |
| `BasketExitEnabled` | Boolean | `true` | When `false`, positions managed individually — no basket break-even logic |
| `EmergencyFlattenEnabled` | Boolean | `true` | **Never set to false in production.** Safety backstop. |

### 9.3 Optimizer Behavior Rules

```
IF RecoveryEnabled = false:
    EXCLUDE from optimization parameter space:
        - MaxRecoverySteps
        - RecoveryMultiplier
        - RecoveryDistance_Min_Points
        - RecoveryCooldown_Seconds
        - BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count
        - StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct
        - [All Trigger parameters]

IF HedgeEnabled = false:
    EXCLUDE from optimization parameter space:
        - MaxHedgeLayers
        - MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier
        - MaxHedgeRatio
        - HedgeCooldown_Seconds
        - HedgeTimeout_Seconds
        - EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct
        - PartialHedgeDD_Pct
        - [All Hedge Trigger parameters]
```

### 9.4 Gate Validation at EA Startup

At EA initialization, the following checks are performed in order:

```
1. Read RecoveryEnabled → Initialize or skip Recovery module
2. Read HedgeEnabled    → Initialize or skip Hedge module
3. IF RecoveryEnabled AND RecoveryMode == NONE:
       WARN: "RecoveryEnabled=true but RecoveryMode=NONE — no recovery will execute"
4. IF HedgeEnabled AND HedgeMode == OFF:
       WARN: "HedgeEnabled=true but HedgeMode=OFF — no hedge will execute"
5. IF HedgeEnabled AND NOT BrokerSupportsHedging:
       ERROR: "Broker does not support hedging. Set HedgeEnabled=false."
       HALT.
6. IF CloseAllAtDD_Pct <= StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct:
       ERROR: "CloseAllAtDD must be greater than StopRecoveryAtDD."
       HALT.
7. IF EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct <= EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct:
       WARN: "EmergencyFlatten fires before Emergency Hedge can unwind. Review thresholds."
```

---

<a name="section-10"></a>
## SECTION 10 — Configuration Templates

### Template 1: Trend Following EA

```yaml
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
# EA Profile: Trend Following EA
# Examples: EMA Crossover, MACD Trend, ADX Trend
# Philosophy: Follow trend, recover on retracement
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

feature_gates:
  RecoveryEnabled: true
  HedgeEnabled: true
  BasketExitEnabled: true

recovery:
  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE
  Triggers:
    - type: DISTANCE
      method: ATR_MULTIPLIER
      atr_period: 14
      atr_multiplier: 1.2
    - type: TREND
      method: EMA_ALIGNMENT
      ema_fast: 20
      ema_slow: 50
      require_both: true          # Both distance AND trend must trigger

  safety:
    MaxRecoverySteps: 3
    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5
    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 30
    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300
    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600
    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 12.0
    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 22.0

hedge:
  HedgeMode: PARTIAL
  Triggers:
    - type: TREND_REVERSAL
      conditions: [EMA_CROSS, HALFTREND_FLIP]
      require_all: false          # Either condition triggers
    - type: EMERGENCY_DD
      threshold_pct: 15.0

  safety:
    MaxHedgeLayers: 2
    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.7
    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 4.0
    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600
    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 14400
    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 28.0
```

---

### Template 2: HalfTrend EA

```yaml
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
# EA Profile: HalfTrend EA
# Philosophy: Strong trend bias, recover only when
#             HalfTrend signal is still intact
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

feature_gates:
  RecoveryEnabled: true
  HedgeEnabled: true
  BasketExitEnabled: true

recovery:
  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE
  Triggers:
    - type: TREND
      method: HALFTREND_VALID      # Recovery ONLY when HalfTrend still aligned
    - type: CANDLE
      method: PIN_BAR              # Additional candle confirmation
      require_both: false          # HalfTrend valid OR pin bar

  safety:
    MaxRecoverySteps: 2            # Conservative — HalfTrend already filters
    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5
    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 25
    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 600  # Allow one full candle before re-evaluation
    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 7200
    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 12.0
    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 22.0

hedge:
  HedgeMode: TREND
  Triggers:
    - type: TREND_REVERSAL
      conditions: [HALFTREND_FLIP]  # Single source of truth for trend direction
    - type: EMERGENCY_DD
      threshold_pct: 15.0

  safety:
    MaxHedgeLayers: 1             # HalfTrend EAs — one hedge layer is sufficient
    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.8
    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 3.0
    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 900
    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 28800   # 8 hours — HalfTrend resolves on higher TF
    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 28.0
```

---

### Template 3: SMC EA

```yaml
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
# EA Profile: SMC (Smart Money Concepts) EA
# Philosophy: Structure-based entries; recover at
#             key structure levels; hedge on CHoCH
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

feature_gates:
  RecoveryEnabled: true
  HedgeEnabled: true
  BasketExitEnabled: true

recovery:
  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE
  Triggers:
    - type: STRUCTURE
      method: HIGHER_LOW           # BUY recovery at confirmed HL
    - type: STRUCTURE
      method: LOWER_HIGH           # SELL recovery at confirmed LH
    - type: DISTANCE
      method: ATR_MULTIPLIER
      atr_period: 14
      atr_multiplier: 1.5
    - logic: AND                   # Structure trigger AND distance trigger required

  safety:
    MaxRecoverySteps: 2            # SMC structure limits valid recovery zones
    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5
    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 40
    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 900
    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 7200
    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 10.0   # Tighter — SMC entries are high-conviction
    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 20.0

hedge:
  HedgeMode: TREND
  Triggers:
    - type: STRUCTURE
      method: CHOCH_CONFIRMED       # CHoCH = strong evidence of reversal
    - type: STRUCTURE
      method: BOS_OPPOSITE          # BOS in opposite direction
    - type: EMERGENCY_DD
      threshold_pct: 12.0

  safety:
    MaxHedgeLayers: 2
    MaxHedgeRatio: 1.0            # Full hedge on confirmed CHoCH
    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 4.0
    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600
    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 21600   # 6 hours
    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 25.0
```

---

### Template 4: Grid EA

```yaml
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
# EA Profile: Grid EA
# Philosophy: Grid is inherently a recovery system.
#             Recovery mode reinforces grid logic.
#             Hedge used only for emergency protection.
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

feature_gates:
  RecoveryEnabled: true
  HedgeEnabled: true              # Emergency only
  BasketExitEnabled: true

recovery:
  RecoveryMode: CONTROLLED        # Grid = controlled distance + lot progression
  Triggers:
    - type: DISTANCE
      method: FIXED_POINTS
      fixed_points: 50            # Grid step size

  safety:
    MaxRecoverySteps: 5           # Grid allows more steps by design
    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.0       # Equal lot grid (set >1.0 for martingale grid)
    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 50
    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 0   # No cooldown — grid entries are immediate
    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: false # Grid allows multiple pairs simultaneously
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 3
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600
    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 20.0   # Grid tolerates higher DD by design
    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 35.0

hedge:
  HedgeMode: EMERGENCY            # Grid: hedge is emergency-only
  Triggers:
    - type: EMERGENCY_DD
      threshold_pct: 20.0         # Higher threshold — grid expects higher DD

  safety:
    MaxHedgeLayers: 1
    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.5            # Partial — full hedge disrupts grid logic
    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 5.0
    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 1800
    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 43200  # 12 hours — grid resolves slowly
    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 40.0
```

---

### Template 5: Mean Reversion EA

```yaml
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
# EA Profile: Mean Reversion EA
# Examples: Bollinger Band bounce, RSI extreme
# Philosophy: Recover during over-extension;
#             hedge only if trend is confirmed (rare)
# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

feature_gates:
  RecoveryEnabled: true
  HedgeEnabled: false             # Mean reversion assumes range — hedge conflicts
  BasketExitEnabled: true

recovery:
  RecoveryMode: CONTROLLED
  Triggers:
    - type: VOLATILITY
      method: ATR_CONTRACTION     # Recover during low-volatility consolidation
    - type: DD
      method: FLOATING_DD
      threshold_pct: 5.0          # Earlier recovery — MR positions revert faster
    - logic: OR

  safety:
    MaxRecoverySteps: 3
    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5
    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 20
    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300
    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 3
    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600
    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 10.0   # Tight — if MR fails it fails fast
    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 18.0

# hedge: disabled — HedgeEnabled: false
# All hedge parameters are ignored by the engine
```

<a name="section-11"></a>
## SECTION 11 — EA Platform Integration

### 11.1 Integration Architecture

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                    EA PLATFORM — SYSTEM INTEGRATION MAP                  ║
║                                                                          ║
║  ┌──────────────┐     Signal      ┌──────────────────────────────────┐   ║
║  │ SIGNAL ENGINE│────────────────►│         ENTRY ENGINE             │   ║
║  │              │                 │  Blocked by Recovery/Hedge state  │   ║
║  └──────────────┘                 └──────────────┬───────────────────┘   ║
║                                                  │ position opened       ║
║  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────►│                       ║
║  │                                              ▼                        ║
║  │   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    ║
║  │   │            RECOVERY & HEDGE ENGINE (this skill)              │    ║
║  │   │                                                              │    ║
║  │   │   ┌─────────────────┐     ┌──────────────────────────────┐  │    ║
║  │   │   │  RECOVERY MODULE │     │       HEDGE MODULE           │  │    ║
║  │   │   │  - Trigger eval  │     │  - Trigger eval              │  │    ║
║  │   │   │  - Safety check  │◄───►│  - Safety check              │  │    ║
║  │   │   │  - Order gen     │     │  - Order gen                 │  │    ║
║  │   │   │  - Basket mgmt   │     │  - Hedge unwind              │  │    ║
║  │   │   └────────┬─────────┘     └──────────────┬───────────────┘  │    ║
║  │   └────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┘    ║
║  │                │                             │                        ║
║  │     Orders     ▼                             ▼  Orders                ║
║  │   ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐      ║
║  │   │                     EXIT ENGINE                            │      ║
║  │   │  - Basket break-even exits (Recovery)                      │      ║
║  │   │  - Individual position exits (Signal TP/SL)                │      ║
║  │   │  - Hedge unwind exits                                      │      ║
║  │   │  - Emergency flatten (Risk Engine command)                 │      ║
║  │   └──────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┘     ║
║  │                                  │ position state                     ║
║  │   ┌──────────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────┐     ║
║  │   │                     RISK ENGINE                             │     ║
║  │   │  - AccountDD monitoring                                    │     ║
║  │   │  - CloseAllAtDD (override all engines)                     │     ║
║  │   │  - Position sizing authority                               │     ║
║  │   │  - Emits: DD_THRESHOLD_BREACH, EMERGENCY_FLATTEN           │     ║
║  │   └──────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┘     ║
║  │                                  │                                    ║
║  │   ┌──────────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────┐     ║
║  │   │                   BASKET ENGINE                             │     ║
║  │   │  - Tracks all open positions by chain ID                   │     ║
║  │   │  - Calculates weighted average entry (break-even)          │     ║
║  │   │  - Separates ORIGINAL, RECOVERY, HEDGE position groups     │     ║
║  │   │  - Emits basket state to all consuming engines             │     ║
║  │   └──────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┘     ║
║  │                                  │                                    ║
║  │   ┌──────────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────┐     ║
║  │   │               DIAGNOSTICS & LOGGING                        │     ║
║  │   │  - All engine events logged with timestamp and state       │     ║
║  │   │  - Recovery chain lifecycle: OPEN, STEP_N, CLOSED, FAILED  │     ║
║  │   │  - Hedge lifecycle: PLACED, TIMEOUT, UNWOUND, EMERGENCY    │     ║
║  │   │  - Safety rule violations: BLOCKED with reason code        │     ║
║  │   │  - Authority overrides: logged with source and target      │     ║
║  │   └──────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┘     ║
║  │                                  │                                    ║
║  └──────────────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────────  ║
║                          PORTFOLIO GUARDIAN                               ║
║          - Cross-symbol DD aggregation                                    ║
║          - Correlated exposure monitoring                                 ║
║          - Portfolio-level hedge trigger (PORTFOLIO trigger)              ║
║          - Emits portfolio events to Risk Engine                          ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
```

---

### 11.2 Integration Contracts

#### Signal Engine → Recovery & Hedge Engine

| Event | Direction | Payload |
|---|---|---|
| `SIGNAL_GENERATED` | Signal → R&H | `{symbol, direction, strength, trigger_type}` |
| `SIGNAL_INVALIDATED` | Signal → R&H | `{symbol, reason}` → may trigger Trend Recovery re-eval |
| `TREND_SCORE_UPDATE` | Signal → R&H | `{symbol, score, direction}` → used by Trend Recovery triggers |

#### Entry Engine → Recovery & Hedge Engine

| Event | Direction | Payload |
|---|---|---|
| `POSITION_OPENED` | Entry → R&H | `{ticket, symbol, direction, lot, entry_price, chain_id}` |
| `ENTRY_BLOCKED` | Entry → R&H | `{reason}` → R&H state caused block — logged |

#### Recovery & Hedge Engine → Entry Engine

| Event | Direction | Payload |
|---|---|---|
| `BLOCK_NEW_SIGNALS` | R&H → Entry | `{reason: "RECOVERY_CHAIN_ACTIVE"}` |
| `RECOVERY_ORDER_REQUEST` | R&H → Entry | `{symbol, direction, lot, type, chain_id, step}` |
| `HEDGE_ORDER_REQUEST` | R&H → Entry | `{symbol, direction, lot, mode, layer, hedge_id}` |

#### Recovery & Hedge Engine → Exit Engine

| Event | Direction | Payload |
|---|---|---|
| `BASKET_EXIT_REQUEST` | R&H → Exit | `{chain_id, tickets[], reason: "BREAK_EVEN_HIT"}` |
| `HEDGE_UNWIND_REQUEST` | R&H → Exit | `{hedge_id, tickets[], reason: "CONDITION_CLEARED | TIMEOUT"}` |
| `EMERGENCY_FLATTEN` | Risk → Exit (via R&H) | `{reason, dd_pct}` — closes all |

#### Basket Engine → Recovery & Hedge Engine

| Data | Direction | Usage |
|---|---|---|
| `BasketBreakEven` | Basket → R&H | Break-even price for basket exit target |
| `NetExposureLots` | Basket → R&H | Used for `MaxHedgeExposure` validation |
| `BasketFloatingPL` | Basket → R&H | DD calculation inputs |
| `ChainPositions[]` | Basket → R&H | Recovery step count, lot distribution |

#### Diagnostics & Logging Contract

Every engine action is logged in a standardized format:

```
[TIMESTAMP] [ENGINE] [EVENT_TYPE] [SYMBOL] [DETAIL]

Example:
2026.06.06 14:00:01  RECOVERY  STEP_PLACED     EURUSD  Step=2, Lot=0.023, Entry=1.08420, Chain=C001
2026.06.06 14:05:22  SAFETY    BLOCKED         EURUSD  Rule=MaxRecoverySteps, Value=3, Limit=3
2026.06.06 14:15:44  HEDGE     EMERGENCY_PLACED EURUSD  Mode=EMERGENCY, Lot=0.10, DD=15.3%
2026.06.06 14:22:11  RISK      FLATTEN_ALL     ALL     DD=25.2%, Threshold=25.0%
2026.06.06 14:22:12  EXIT      FLATTEN_EXEC    EURUSD  Tickets=[1001,1002,1003], Reason=EMERGENCY
```

---

### 11.3 Portfolio Guardian Integration

```
Portfolio Guardian monitors at the ACCOUNT level:

┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    PORTFOLIO GUARDIAN                      │
│                                                            │
│  Inputs:                                                   │
│    ─ All open positions (symbol, lot, direction, PL)       │
│    ─ Correlation matrix (cross-symbol)                     │
│    ─ Account balance, equity, margin                       │
│                                                            │
│  Outputs to Risk Engine:                                   │
│    ─ PortfolioDD_Pct (total floating / equity)             │
│    ─ CorrelatedExposure_Pct (% of portfolio in correlated) │
│    ─ MarginUtilization_Pct                                 │
│                                                            │
│  Outputs to Hedge Engine (Portfolio trigger):              │
│    ─ PORTFOLIO_HEDGE_SIGNAL when correlation or DD         │
│      thresholds breached                                   │
│                                                            │
│  Authority: Portfolio Guardian reports TO Risk Engine.     │
│  It does not place trades. It does not modify positions.   │
│  Its only output is signals and metrics.                   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## Appendix A — Parameter Quick Reference

### Recovery Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RecoveryEnabled` | Bool | `false` | — | Master gate |
| `RecoveryMode` | Enum | `NONE` | NONE/SOFT/ADAPTIVE/CONTROLLED | Operating mode |
| `MaxRecoverySteps` | Int | `3` | 1–5 | Max recovery orders per chain |
| `RecoveryMultiplier` | Float | `1.5` | 1.0–2.0 | Lot multiplier per step |
| `RecoveryDistance_Min_Points` | Int | `20` | 10–100 | Min distance between entries |
| `RecoveryCooldown_Seconds` | Int | `300` | 60–1800 | Min time between steps |
| `OneRecoveryChainAtATime` | Bool | `true` | — | Portfolio chain limit |
| `BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count` | Int | `2` | 1–5 | Loss count to trigger block |
| `BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds` | Int | `3600` | 1800–86400 | Block duration |
| `StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct` | Float | `15.0` | 5–25 | DD that suspends recovery |
| `CloseAllAtDD_Pct` | Float | `25.0` | 15–40 | DD that closes all positions |

### Hedge Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `HedgeEnabled` | Bool | `false` | — | Master gate |
| `HedgeMode` | Enum | `OFF` | OFF/EMERGENCY/PARTIAL/BREAKOUT/TREND/RECOVERY | Operating mode |
| `MaxHedgeLayers` | Int | `2` | 1–3 | Max simultaneous hedge positions |
| `MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier` | Float | `5.0` | 1.0–10.0 | Max hedge lots = Base × mult |
| `MaxHedgeRatio` | Float | `1.0` | 0.3–1.0 | Max hedge as fraction of exposure |
| `HedgeCooldown_Seconds` | Int | `600` | 300–3600 | Min time between hedge orders |
| `HedgeTimeout_Seconds` | Int | `14400` | 3600–86400 | Auto-unwind timeout |
| `EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct` | Float | `30.0` | 20–50 | DD that closes all (incl. hedges) |
| `NoRecursiveHedge` | Bool | `true` | enforced | Cannot hedge a hedge position |

---

## Appendix B — Error Codes

| Code | Engine | Meaning | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| `REC_001` | Recovery | MaxRecoverySteps exceeded | Hold position. Log. |
| `REC_002` | Recovery | RecoveryMultiplier would exceed MaxLotSize | Reduce lot to cap or block. |
| `REC_003` | Recovery | RecoveryCooldown active | Queue. Retry after cooldown. |
| `REC_004` | Recovery | OneRecoveryChainAtATime — chain already active | Block. Log. |
| `REC_005` | Recovery | BlockRecoveryAfterLoss active | Block. Log. Resume after cooldown. |
| `REC_006` | Recovery | StopRecoveryAtDD threshold breached | Suspend all recovery. |
| `REC_007` | Recovery | Hedge engine suspended recovery | Log. Resume on HEDGE_RESOLVED. |
| `HDG_001` | Hedge | MaxHedgeLayers exceeded | Block new hedge. |
| `HDG_002` | Hedge | MaxHedgeRatio exceeded | Reduce lot to ratio cap. |
| `HDG_003` | Hedge | HedgeCooldown active | Queue. Retry after cooldown. |
| `HDG_004` | Hedge | NoRecursiveHedge — target is hedge position | Block. Log error. |
| `HDG_005` | Hedge | HedgeTimeout expired | Auto-unwind hedge position. |
| `HDG_006` | Hedge | Broker does not support hedging | Startup halt. |
| `RISK_001` | Risk | CloseAllAtDD triggered | Emergency flatten all. Halt EA. |
| `RISK_002` | Risk | EmergencyFlatten triggered | Emergency flatten all. Halt EA. |
| `CFG_001` | Config | CloseAllAtDD ≤ StopRecoveryAtDD | Config error. Startup halt. |
| `CFG_002` | Config | EmergencyFlatten ≤ EmergencyHedge threshold | Config warning. |
| `CFG_003` | Config | HedgeEnabled but broker netting mode | Startup halt. |

---

## Appendix C — Glossary

| Term | Definition |
|---|---|
| **Recovery Chain** | A group of positions (original + recovery entries) managed together as one basket |
| **Chain ID** | Unique identifier assigned to a recovery chain (`C001`, `C002`...) |
| **Break-Even** | Weighted average entry price of all positions in the basket |
| **BaseLot** | The original lot size placed by the Signal Engine |
| **Recovery Step** | Each additional recovery order added after the original entry (Step 1, 2, 3...) |
| **Hedge Layer** | One hedge position (multiple layers = multiple hedge positions) |
| **Hedge Ratio** | `HedgeLot / NetExposureLot` — fraction of exposure neutralized |
| **Net Exposure** | Sum of all BUY lots minus sum of all SELL lots for a symbol |
| **Feature Gate** | A boolean parameter that enables or disables an entire engine module |
| **Authority** | The rank of an engine in the hierarchy that determines who can override whom |
| **Recursive Hedge** | A hedge placed on a position that is already a hedge — prohibited |
| **Basket Exit** | Closing all positions in a recovery chain simultaneously at break-even |
| **DD** | Drawdown — expressed as floating loss / account balance × 100 |
| **ATR** | Average True Range — volatility measure used for dynamic distance calculations |
| **BOS** | Break of Structure — SMC concept: price breaks a prior swing high or low |
| **CHoCH** | Change of Character — SMC concept: structure switches from bullish to bearish or vice versa |
| **HL** | Higher Low — bullish structure: each pullback ends above the previous low |
| **LH** | Lower High — bearish structure: each rally ends below the previous high |
