<details type="tool_calls" done="true" id="toolu_016UnhkRs9AEHvdd6Zep3xS6" name="SCGC-doc-gen-mcp_generate_word" arguments="&quot;{\&quot;title\&quot;: \&quot;recovery-and-hedge-engine\&quot;, \&quot;category_label\&quot;: \&quot;EA LAB \\u00b7 PLATFORM SKILL \\u00b7 v1.0.0\&quot;, \&quot;subtitle\&quot;: \&quot;Universal Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine \\u2014 Strategy-Agnostic Specification\&quot;, \&quot;date_label\&quot;: \&quot;June 2026 \\u00b7 EA Lab Architecture Team\&quot;, \&quot;author\&quot;: \&quot;EA Lab Architecture Team | June 2026\&quot;, \&quot;content\&quot;: \&quot;# Overview\\n\\n**Version:** 1.0.0 | **Status:** Production-Ready | **Scope:** Universal \\u2014 Strategy-Agnostic\\n\\nThis skill defines the canonical Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine for the EA Lab platform. It is reusable across all EA strategies: Trend Following, HalfTrend, Breakout, SMC, Mean Reversion, Grid, and Hybrid systems. No section of this specification assumes any particular signal logic.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 1 \\u2014 Design Philosophy\\n\\n## Recovery Engine vs Hedge Engine\\n\\nThese are two fundamentally different systems. They must never be conflated, chained implicitly, or assumed to co-activate.\\n\\n**Recovery Engine**\\n\\n- Objective: Improve average entry price\\n- Mechanism: Add positions in the SAME direction as the loss\\n- Outcome: Reduce break-even distance, coordinate basket exits\\n- Risk: Increases total exposure\\n\\n*\\\&quot;I am losing. I will improve my position to escape faster.\\\&quot;*\\n\\n**Hedge Engine**\\n\\n- Objective: Reduce portfolio exposure\\n- Mechanism: Add positions in the OPPOSITE direction\\n- Outcome: Reduce drawdown, survive adverse movement\\n- Risk: Locks in spread cost and opportunity cost\\n\\n*\\\&quot;I am losing. I will neutralize exposure to survive this move.\\\&quot;*\\n\\n## Independence Rules\\n\\n| Rule | Recovery Engine | Hedge Engine |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| May activate without the other | Yes | Yes |\\n| May trigger the other | Never | Never |\\n| Assumes the other is active | Never | Never |\\n| May block the other | Yes \\u2014 via Authority Model | Yes \\u2014 via Authority Model |\\n| Shares position sizing | No \\u2014 Independent | No \\u2014 Independent |\\n| Shares exit logic | No \\u2014 Independent | No \\u2014 Independent |\\n\\n## Strategy Compatibility Matrix\\n\\n| EA Strategy | Recovery | Hedge | Notes |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| Trend Following | Yes | Yes | Recovery follows trend direction |\\n| HalfTrend | Yes | Yes | Recovery on HalfTrend confirmation |\\n| Breakout | Yes | Yes | Hedge on false breakout |\\n| SMC | Yes | Yes | Recovery at BOS/CHoCH levels |\\n| Mean Reversion | Yes | Caution | Hedge conflicts with MR philosophy |\\n| Grid | Yes | Caution | Grid is inherently recovery; hedge rarely needed |\\n| Hybrid | Yes | Yes | Requires careful authority configuration |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 2 \\u2014 Recovery Engine\\n\\n## Recovery Modes\\n\\n### MODE: NONE\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Objective | Disable recovery entirely. EA operates single-position only. |\\n| Risk Profile | Baseline \\u2014 no additional exposure added |\\n| Intended Use | Conservative EAs, during optimization, or when capital is constrained |\\n| Advantages | Cleanest risk profile. No compounding exposure. Backtest results are most predictable. Optimizer can properly isolate signal quality. |\\n| Failure Modes | A losing position has no recovery path. Single trade losses directly impact account equity. May cause unnecessary stops on valid setups. |\\n\\n### MODE: SOFT\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Objective | Place a single recovery order at a fixed distance. Simple and predictable. |\\n| Risk Profile | Low-to-Moderate. Maximum 1 additional order per chain. |\\n| Intended Use | Trend Following, HalfTrend, Breakout EAs where brief retracements are common |\\n| Advantages | Minimal complexity. Easy to backtest. Break-even distance cut by approximately 50% on successful recovery. |\\n| Failure Modes | Only one recovery attempt. If price continues beyond recovery entry, exposure doubles with no further mitigation. |\\n\\n*Example flow:* Original Entry BUY @ 1.3000 \\u2192 Move -50 pts \\u2192 Recovery BUY @ 1.2950 \\u2192 Basket Break-Even = 1.2975\\n\\n### MODE: ADAPTIVE\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Objective | Place recovery orders at dynamic distances based on market volatility (ATR). Scale lot size proportionally to maintain consistent break-even distance. |\\n| Risk Profile | Moderate. Exposure scales with step count but remains volatility-calibrated. |\\n| Intended Use | SMC, HalfTrend, Hybrid EAs operating across variable-volatility instruments |\\n| Advantages | Self-adjusting to market conditions. Avoids premature recovery in high-volatility environments. Works well in trending markets. |\\n| Failure Modes | ATR can expand dramatically in news events. Complexity makes manual intervention harder. |\\n\\n*Calculation:* Recovery Distance = ATR(Period) x ATR_Multiplier | Recovery Lot = BaseLot x RecoveryMultiplier^step | Break-Even = Sum(Lot_i x Entry_i) / Sum(Lot_i)\\n\\n### MODE: CONTROLLED\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Objective | Full recovery sequence with hard caps on steps and multiplier ceiling. Structured martingale with enforced limits. |\\n| Risk Profile | Moderate-to-High. Maximum exposure is deterministic and pre-calculated. |\\n| Intended Use | Grid EAs, Mean Reversion EAs, Hybrid systems where recovery sequences are core to the strategy |\\n| Advantages | Maximum exposure is known before trade entry. Break-even analysis is deterministic. Safety caps prevent runaway exposure. |\\n| Failure Modes | Maximum steps can still produce significant drawdown if market trends strongly. A full chain failure produces the largest single loss of all modes. |\\n\\n**CONTROLLED Pre-Calculation Table (BaseLot = 0.01):**\\n\\n| Step | Multiplier 1.0x | Multiplier 1.5x | Multiplier 2.0x |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| 0 (original) | 0.01 | 0.01 | 0.01 |\\n| 1 | 0.01 | 0.015 | 0.02 |\\n| 2 | 0.01 | 0.023 | 0.04 |\\n| 3 | 0.01 | 0.034 | 0.08 |\\n| 4 | 0.01 | 0.051 | 0.16 \\u2014 BLOCKED by MaxLot |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 3 \\u2014 Recovery Triggers\\n\\n## Trigger Type 1: Distance Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Formula | Default |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| Fixed Points | CurrentPrice - EntryPrice &gt; FixedPoints | 50 pts |\\n| ATR-Based | CurrentPrice - EntryPrice &gt; ATR(14) | 1x ATR(14) |\\n| ATR Multiplier | CurrentPrice - EntryPrice &gt; ATR(14) x Multiplier | 1.5x ATR(14) |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Simple, deterministic, easy to backtest. No indicator dependency. Fires reliably. |\\n| Weaknesses | Ignores market structure. May fire during normal retracements of a valid trend. Fixed distance becomes irrelevant across instruments with different volatility profiles. |\\n| Best Use Cases | SOFT mode on Trend Following and HalfTrend EAs. Grid EAs where fixed levels are part of strategy design. Low-volatility instruments. |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 2: Candle Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| Engulfing | Prior candle body fully engulfed by current candle in recovery direction |\\n| Pin Bar | Shadow &gt;= 2x body length, close &gt;= 60% of range in recovery direction |\\n| Strong Close | Close in top/bottom 25% of candle range AND range &gt;= 1.0x ATR(14) |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Adds confluence to recovery decision. Reduces recovery entries at arbitrary distances. Aligns with price action logic in many signal engines. |\\n| Weaknesses | Prone to false signals in consolidating markets. May delay recovery beyond optimal entry point. Sensitive to broker feed quality. |\\n| Best Use Cases | SMC EAs, HalfTrend EAs. ADAPTIVE mode on higher timeframes (H1, H4). |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 3: Trend Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| HalfTrend Valid | HalfTrend indicator still points in original trade direction |\\n| EMA Alignment | EMA(fast) still above/below EMA(slow) in trade direction |\\n| Trend Score | Internal trend score &gt;= defined threshold (e.g. &gt;= 60/100) |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Highest quality recovery entries. Ensures recovery only occurs in a directionally valid context. Prevents recovery into a full trend reversal. |\\n| Weaknesses | If trend invalidates before recovery threshold, recovery is blocked \\u2014 position may be left unmanaged. Lagging indicators may confirm trend validity too late. |\\n| Best Use Cases | Trend Following EAs, HalfTrend EAs. ADAPTIVE and CONTROLLED modes. Medium-to-long timeframes (H4, D1). |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 4: Structure Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| BOS | Price breaks the most recent swing high/low in recovery direction |\\n| CHoCH | HH to HL or LH to LL pattern shift detected in recovery direction |\\n| Higher Low (HL) | For BUY recovery: confirmed Higher Low on chart |\\n| Lower High (LH) | For SELL recovery: confirmed Lower High on chart |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Institutionally validated entry levels. Recovery aligns with Smart Money logic. High-probability entry context. |\\n| Weaknesses | Structure breaks can be false (stop hunts, liquidity grabs). Fewer triggers in low-structure environments. |\\n| Best Use Cases | SMC EAs, Breakout EAs. ADAPTIVE mode. H1\\u2013H4 timeframes. |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 5: DD Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| Floating DD % | FloatingLoss / AccountBalance &gt; Threshold_% |\\n| Cycle DD % | CycleLoss / CycleStartBalance &gt; Threshold_% |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Directly tied to account impact. Prevents recovery from triggering on minor price noise. Ensures recovery is proportional to actual financial stress. |\\n| Weaknesses | Delayed trigger relative to price-based methods. In fast markets, DD may exceed threshold before recovery executes. |\\n| Best Use Cases | CONTROLLED mode. Grid and Mean Reversion EAs where DD thresholds are pre-defined strategy parameters. |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 6: Volatility Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| ATR Expansion | ATR(current) &gt; ATR(reference) x Expansion_Multiplier |\\n| ATR Contraction | ATR(current) &lt; ATR(reference) x Contraction_Multiplier |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | ATR Expansion: recovery during high-momentum moves where trend continuation is likely. ATR Contraction: recovery during consolidation where mean reversion is most predictable. |\\n| Weaknesses | ATR is a lagging indicator. Expansion can signal an already-exhausted trend. |\\n| Best Use Cases | Expansion: Breakout, Trend Following. Contraction: Mean Reversion, Grid. Both: Hybrid systems adapting to regime. |\\n\\n## Trigger Comparison Matrix\\n\\n| Trigger | Speed | Noise Resistance | Structure Awareness | Best Mode |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| Distance | Fast | Low | None | SOFT, CONTROLLED |\\n| Candle | Medium | Medium | Medium | ADAPTIVE |\\n| Trend | Medium | High | Low | ADAPTIVE |\\n| Structure | Slow | High | High | ADAPTIVE |\\n| DD | Slow | High | None | CONTROLLED |\\n| Volatility | Medium | Medium | None | ADAPTIVE, CONTROLLED |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 4 \\u2014 Recovery Safety Rules\\n\\nAll safety rules are evaluated BEFORE any recovery order is submitted. A single rule violation blocks the entire recovery action.\\n\\n## Rule 1: MaxRecoverySteps\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer |\\n| Default | 3 |\\n| Recommended Range | 1 \\u2013 5 |\\n| Function | Hard ceiling on the number of recovery orders in a single chain. Step 0 = original entry. Step 1\\u2013N = recovery entries. |\\n| On Violation | Recovery blocked. Position held. Basket managed by Exit Engine only. |\\n| Warning | Setting &gt; 5 produces exponential lot growth. Never exceed unless strategy is explicitly designed for it. |\\n\\n## Rule 2: RecoveryMultiplier\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float |\\n| Default | 1.5 |\\n| Recommended Range | 1.0 \\u2013 2.0 |\\n| Function | Lot size multiplier at each recovery step. RecoveryLot_N = BaseLot x RecoveryMultiplier^N |\\n| On Violation | If computed lot exceeds MaxLotSize, recovery is blocked. |\\n| Warning | Multiplier of 2.0 over 3 steps = 8x base lot. Always pre-calculate maximum exposure before deployment. |\\n\\n## Rule 3: RecoveryDistance\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (points) |\\n| Default | 20 pts (5-digit brokers) |\\n| Recommended Range | 10 \\u2013 100 pts depending on instrument |\\n| Function | Minimum distance between existing recovery entry and proposed new recovery entry. Prevents stacking orders too close together. |\\n| On Violation | Recovery order deferred until price reaches minimum separation. |\\n\\n## Rule 4: RecoveryCooldown\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (seconds) |\\n| Default | 300 seconds |\\n| Recommended Range | 60 \\u2013 1800 seconds |\\n| Function | Minimum elapsed time between consecutive recovery entries within the same chain. Prevents multiple recovery orders during rapid price movement (e.g. news spike). |\\n\\n## Rule 5: OneRecoveryChainAtATime\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Boolean |\\n| Default | true |\\n| Function | If a recovery chain is active on Symbol A, no new recovery chain may be started on any other position until Symbol A&#x27;s chain is resolved. |\\n| Rationale | Prevents compounding exposure across multiple simultaneous recovery chains. Portfolio-level risk control. |\\n\\n## Rule 6: BlockRecoveryAfterLoss\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (count) |\\n| Default | 2 consecutive chain losses |\\n| Cooldown | BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds = 3600 |\\n| Function | Block recovery for the cooldown period after N consecutive chain losses. Recovery module enters cooling state \\u2014 all requests rejected until timer expires. |\\n| Rationale | Consecutive chain losses indicate regime incompatibility. Prevents continued exposure amplification into a losing market condition. |\\n\\n## Rule 7: StopRecoveryAtDD\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float (percentage) |\\n| Default | 15.0% |\\n| Function | If account floating DD exceeds threshold, all recovery activities are suspended immediately. No new recovery orders may be placed. |\\n| Behavior | Recovery module freezes. Basket held. Exit Engine takes full control. Hedge Engine may activate per its own authority. |\\n| Rationale | At 15% DD, the priority is capital preservation \\u2014 not break-even recovery. |\\n\\n## Rule 8: CloseAllAtDD\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float (percentage) |\\n| Default | 25.0% |\\n| Function | If account floating DD exceeds this hard threshold, ALL positions are closed immediately \\u2014 including any active recovery chain. |\\n| Behavior | Emergency flatten. Overrides all engines. EA enters HALTED state until manual reset. |\\n| Rationale | Last line of defense. Ensures the account is never at risk of margin call due to unmanaged recovery exposure. |\\n\\n## Default Configuration Summary\\n\\n```yaml\\nrecovery_safety:\\n  MaxRecoverySteps: 3\\n  RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n  RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 20\\n  RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300\\n  OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n  BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2\\n  BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600\\n  StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 15.0\\n  CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 25.0\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 5 \\u2014 Hedge Engine\\n\\n## Hedge Modes\\n\\n### MODE: OFF\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Hedge engine is entirely inactive. No hedge positions are opened under any condition. |\\n| When to Use | Conservative strategies, single-currency EAs, or when broker conditions do not support hedging. |\\n| Effect | All hedge triggers are ignored. HedgeEnabled=false enforces this mode regardless of other settings. |\\n\\n### MODE: EMERGENCY\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Activated when account DD exceeds EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct. Places a hedge equal to or near the full net open exposure immediately \\u2014 no signal confirmation required. |\\n| Trigger | DD-based. Automatic. Highest priority within Hedge Engine. |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 1.0 (100% \\u2014 full neutralization) |\\n| Exit Condition | Price recovery to HedgeBreakEven_Buffer OR HedgeTimeout expires |\\n| Risk | Locks in current loss level. Spread cost on unwind. Opportunity cost if market reverses favorably. |\\n| Rationale | Survival. Stop the bleeding. Evaluate after hedge is placed. |\\n\\n### MODE: PARTIAL\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Activated by moderate DD or exposure threshold. Places a partial hedge covering a defined percentage of net exposure. |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.3 \\u2013 0.7 (configurable) |\\n| Risk | Does not fully protect \\u2014 adverse movement beyond hedge ratio still produces losses. |\\n| Rationale | Reduce delta without committing to full neutralization. Retain upside potential. |\\n\\n### MODE: BREAKOUT\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Hedge placed in the direction of a confirmed breakout, counter to the original trade direction. Designed to survive a false breakout that has now confirmed as genuine. |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 (based on breakout strength) |\\n| Risk | If breakout reverses, hedge generates a loss added to original position loss. Double jeopardy. |\\n| Rationale | When a breakout is confirmed against the position, a hedge allows survival and potential profit on the new direction. |\\n\\n### MODE: TREND\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Hedge placed when trend analysis indicates a confirmed reversal against the open position. The hedge captures the new trend direction. |\\n| Trigger | Trend reversal signal: EMA crossover, HalfTrend flip, trend score collapse |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 |\\n| Risk | If trend indicator gives a false reversal, both the original position and the hedge are simultaneously losing. |\\n\\n### MODE: RECOVERY\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | A partial or full hedge placed specifically while a recovery chain is active. Neutralizes additional risk added by recovery orders while the chain resolves toward break-even. |\\n| Hedge Ratio | Calculated as: HedgeLot = RecoveryExtraExposure x HedgeRatio_Recovery |\\n| Risk | Recovery hedge reduces break-even improvement \\u2014 must be used cautiously. |\\n| Rationale | Provides a safety ceiling on recovery chain exposure. Maximum loss is bounded. |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 6 \\u2014 Hedge Triggers\\n\\n## Trigger 1: Emergency DD Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | FloatingDD_Pct &gt;= EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct (default: 15%) |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 1.0 (full neutralization) |\\n| Expected Behavior | Immediate market order opposite to net basket direction. All other engines notified. Recovery engine suspended. |\\n| Risk | Position locked. Spread cost on both sides. Opportunity missed if market reverses immediately. |\\n\\n## Trigger 2: Partial Exposure Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | NetExposureLots &gt;= MaxExposureLots_Threshold OR FloatingDD_Pct &gt;= PartialHedgeDD_Pct (default: 8%) |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.3 \\u2013 0.5 |\\n| Expected Behavior | Partial hedge placed. Original position continues. Net delta reduced but not eliminated. |\\n| Risk | Insufficient protection if adverse movement accelerates. May require escalation to Emergency mode. |\\n\\n## Trigger 3: Breakout Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | Price breaks confirmed structure level (BOS) against original trade direction with momentum confirmation |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 (proportional to breakout strength) |\\n| Expected Behavior | Hedge placed in breakout direction. If original trade reaches break-even, original closed. Hedge held independently. |\\n| Risk | Breakout may be a stop hunt \\u2014 hedge position immediately underwater. |\\n\\n## Trigger 4: Trend Reversal Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | EMA_Cross_Confirmed AND HalfTrend_Flip AND TrendScore &lt; 40 |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 0.8 |\\n| Expected Behavior | Hedge placed in new trend direction. Original position held pending signal recovery OR closed at next break-even opportunity. |\\n| Risk | Multiple indicator confirmation still produces false reversals in choppy markets. |\\n\\n## Trigger 5: Candle Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | Strong reversal candle against position: Engulfing candle with close in top/bottom 20% of range AND size &gt;= 1.5x ATR(14) |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.3 \\u2013 0.5 |\\n| Expected Behavior | Quick partial hedge at candle close. Re-evaluated on next candle. If reversal continues, escalate to Trend Reversal Hedge. |\\n| Risk | Single-candle signals have high false-positive rates, especially on lower timeframes. |\\n\\n## Trigger 6: Structure Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | CHoCH confirmed + liquidity pool swept on the opposite side |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 |\\n| Expected Behavior | Hedge placed at CHoCH confirmation. Held until original position can be exited at break-even or defined loss. |\\n| Risk | CHoCH misidentification in complex market conditions. Requires clean price action environment. |\\n\\n## Trigger 7: Portfolio Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | PortfolioDD_Pct &gt;= PortfolioHedgeThreshold_Pct OR CorrelatedExposure_Pct &gt;= CorrelationThreshold_Pct |\\n| Hedge Ratio | Calculated at portfolio level \\u2014 proportional to correlated exposure |\\n| Expected Behavior | Hedge placed on most liquid correlated instrument. Reduces systemic exposure across correlated positions. |\\n| Risk | Correlation assumptions can break down during risk-off events \\u2014 the very moment portfolio hedges are most needed. |\\n\\n## Hedge Trigger Comparison Matrix\\n\\n| Trigger | Speed | Signal Quality | Hedge Ratio | Best Mode |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| Emergency DD | Immediate | N/A (DD-driven) | 1.0 | EMERGENCY |\\n| Partial Exposure | Medium | High | 0.3\\u20130.5 | PARTIAL |\\n| Breakout | Medium | Medium | 0.5\\u20131.0 | BREAKOUT |\\n| Trend Reversal | Slow | High | 0.5\\u20130.8 | TREND |\\n| Candle | Fast | Low\\u2013Medium | 0.3\\u20130.5 | PARTIAL |\\n| Structure | Slow | High | 0.5\\u20131.0 | TREND, RECOVERY |\\n| Portfolio | Medium | High | Variable | PARTIAL, EMERGENCY |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 7 \\u2014 Hedge Safety Rules\\n\\nAll hedge safety rules are evaluated before any hedge order is placed. A single violation blocks the hedge action and logs the rejection.\\n\\n## Rule 1: MaxHedgeLayers\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer |\\n| Default | 2 |\\n| Recommended Range | 1 \\u2013 3 |\\n| Function | Maximum number of simultaneous hedge positions open at any one time (across all modes). |\\n| On Violation | New hedge request blocked. Existing hedge positions managed by Exit Engine. |\\n| Rationale | Multiple hedge layers create complex net exposure that is difficult to manage and unwind cleanly. |\\n\\n## Rule 2: MaxHedgeExposure\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float |\\n| Default | BaseLot x 5.0 |\\n| Function | Maximum total lot size across all active hedge positions. |\\n| On Violation | New hedge order lot is reduced to fit within remaining capacity, or rejected if no capacity remains. |\\n\\n## Rule 3: MaxHedgeRatio\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float |\\n| Default | 1.0 (100% of original net exposure) |\\n| Recommended Range | 0.3 \\u2013 1.0 |\\n| Function | No hedge position may exceed this fraction of the current net original position exposure. |\\n| On Violation | Hedge lot capped at MaxHedgeRatio x NetExposureLots. |\\n| Rationale | Prevents over-hedging \\u2014 a situation where the hedge creates more directional exposure than the original position. |\\n\\n## Rule 4: HedgeCooldown\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (seconds) |\\n| Default | 600 seconds |\\n| Recommended Range | 300 \\u2013 3600 seconds |\\n| Function | Minimum elapsed time between consecutive hedge orders per symbol. Prevents stacking hedge layers in rapid succession during high-volatility events. |\\n\\n## Rule 5: HedgeTimeout\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (seconds) |\\n| Default | 14400 seconds (4 hours) |\\n| Recommended Range | 3600 \\u2013 86400 seconds |\\n| Function | If a hedge position has been open longer than HedgeTimeout_Seconds and has not been unwound by normal conditions, it is automatically closed at market. |\\n| On Trigger | Forced unwind. Logged as HEDGE_TIMEOUT_UNWIND. |\\n| Rationale | Hedges are temporary tools \\u2014 not permanent positions. An open hedge indefinitely accumulates swap cost and locks capital. |\\n\\n## Rule 6: EmergencyFlatten\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float (percentage) |\\n| Default | 30.0% |\\n| Function | If combined account DD from all positions (including active hedges) reaches this threshold, ALL positions are closed immediately. EA enters HALTED state. Manual review required before restart. |\\n| Rationale | Ensures a failed hedge does not compound the original drawdown beyond survival. |\\n\\n## Rule 7: NoRecursiveHedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Boolean (enforced \\u2014 not configurable) |\\n| Default | true (always enforced) |\\n| Function | A hedge position tagged as [HEDGE] cannot itself be hedged. Any attempt is blocked and logged as RECURSIVE_HEDGE_BLOCKED. |\\n| Rationale | Recursive hedging creates circular exposure that grows without bound and cannot be unwound without incurring a defined loss. This rule is non-negotiable. |\\n\\n## Default Configuration Summary\\n\\n```yaml\\nhedge_safety:\\n  MaxHedgeLayers: 2\\n  MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 5.0\\n  MaxHedgeRatio: 1.0\\n  HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600\\n  HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 14400\\n  EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 30.0\\n  NoRecursiveHedge: true\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 8 \\u2014 Authority Model\\n\\n## Authority Hierarchy\\n\\n**TIER 1 \\u2014 RISK ENGINE** *(Highest Authority)*\\n\\n\\\&quot;Account survival is the only non-negotiable rule.\\\&quot;\\n\\n- Sets CloseAllAtDD_Pct and EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct\\n- May override ANY engine at any time\\n- No engine may override Risk Engine\\n\\n**TIER 2 \\u2014 HEDGE ENGINE**\\n\\n\\\&quot;Reduce exposure before it becomes a crisis.\\\&quot;\\n\\n- May suspend Recovery Engine\\n- May reduce Signal Engine&#x27;s new trade permission\\n- Cannot override Risk Engine\\n\\n**TIER 3 \\u2014 RECOVERY ENGINE**\\n\\n\\\&quot;Improve position to escape loss efficiently.\\\&quot;\\n\\n- May queue Signal Engine entries\\n- Cannot override Hedge or Risk Engine\\n- Suspended when Hedge Engine is in EMERGENCY mode\\n\\n**TIER 4 \\u2014 SIGNAL ENGINE** *(Lowest Authority)*\\n\\n\\\&quot;Generate directional trade opportunities.\\\&quot;\\n\\n- New trades blocked during Recovery chain\\n- New trades blocked during EMERGENCY hedge\\n- Provides trigger context to Recovery and Hedge Engines\\n\\n## Authority Rationale\\n\\n| Rule | Reasoning |\\n|---|---|\\n| Risk Engine ranks first | No strategy objective justifies account destruction. A signal may be wrong; risk limits are always right. |\\n| Hedge ranks above Recovery | Recovery increases exposure. If the hedge engine determines that exposure must be reduced, adding recovery orders directly contradicts that objective. |\\n| Recovery ranks above Signal | A recovery chain changes the account&#x27;s position structure. New signals entering while a recovery chain is active would create orphaned positions that interfere with basket management. |\\n| Signal ranks last | The Signal Engine&#x27;s job is to find opportunities. All other engines exist to manage what happens after signals are acted upon. |\\n\\n## Cross-Engine Communication Protocol\\n\\n| Event | Emitter | Receiver | Action |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| DD_THRESHOLD_BREACH | Risk Engine | All Engines | Engines evaluate their suspension conditions |\\n| EMERGENCY_FLATTEN | Risk Engine | All Engines | All positions closed. All queues cleared. |\\n| HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE | Hedge Engine | Recovery Engine | Recovery suspended until HEDGE_RESOLVED |\\n| HEDGE_RESOLVED | Hedge Engine | Recovery Engine | Recovery engine may resume evaluation |\\n| RECOVERY_CHAIN_ACTIVE | Recovery Engine | Signal Engine | New trade entries blocked |\\n| RECOVERY_CHAIN_CLOSED | Recovery Engine | Signal Engine | New trade entries permitted |\\n| RECOVERY_BREAK_EVEN_HIT | Recovery Engine | Exit Engine | Close basket at market |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 9 \\u2014 Feature Gating\\n\\n## Design Principle\\n\\nFeature gating ensures that disabled modules have zero runtime cost, zero side effects, and are completely invisible to the optimizer.\\n\\n&gt; An optimizer running with RecoveryEnabled=false must not consider any recovery parameters in its parameter space \\u2014 as if those parameters do not exist.\\n\\n## Gate Registry\\n\\n| Gate Parameter | Type | Default | Effect When False |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| RecoveryEnabled | Boolean | false | All recovery parameters, triggers, and order logic disabled. Recovery module does not execute. |\\n| HedgeEnabled | Boolean | false | All hedge parameters, triggers, and order logic disabled. Hedge module does not execute. |\\n| RecoveryMode | Enum | NONE | When NONE, same effect as RecoveryEnabled=false with logging |\\n| HedgeMode | Enum | OFF | When OFF, same effect as HedgeEnabled=false with logging |\\n| BasketExitEnabled | Boolean | true | When false, positions managed individually \\u2014 no basket break-even logic |\\n| EmergencyFlattenEnabled | Boolean | true | Never set to false in production. |\\n\\n## Optimizer Exclusion Rules\\n\\nWhen RecoveryEnabled = false, exclude from optimization parameter space:\\n- MaxRecoverySteps, RecoveryMultiplier, RecoveryDistance_Min_Points, RecoveryCooldown_Seconds, BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count, StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct, and all Trigger parameters\\n\\nWhen HedgeEnabled = false, exclude from optimization parameter space:\\n- MaxHedgeLayers, MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier, MaxHedgeRatio, HedgeCooldown_Seconds, HedgeTimeout_Seconds, EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct, PartialHedgeDD_Pct, and all Hedge Trigger parameters\\n\\n## Gate Validation at EA Startup\\n\\nThe following checks run at initialization in order:\\n\\n1. Read RecoveryEnabled \\u2014 Initialize or skip Recovery module\\n2. Read HedgeEnabled \\u2014 Initialize or skip Hedge module\\n3. If RecoveryEnabled AND RecoveryMode == NONE: WARN \\\&quot;RecoveryEnabled=true but RecoveryMode=NONE \\u2014 no recovery will execute\\\&quot;\\n4. If HedgeEnabled AND HedgeMode == OFF: WARN \\\&quot;HedgeEnabled=true but HedgeMode=OFF \\u2014 no hedge will execute\\\&quot;\\n5. If HedgeEnabled AND NOT BrokerSupportsHedging: ERROR \\u2014 HALT\\n6. If CloseAllAtDD_Pct &lt;= StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: ERROR \\\&quot;CloseAllAtDD must be greater than StopRecoveryAtDD\\\&quot; \\u2014 HALT\\n7. If EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct &lt;= EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct: WARN \\\&quot;EmergencyFlatten fires before Emergency Hedge can unwind. Review thresholds.\\\&quot;\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 10 \\u2014 Configuration Templates\\n\\n## Template 1: Trend Following EA\\n\\n```yaml\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: true\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: DISTANCE\\n      method: ATR_MULTIPLIER\\n      atr_period: 14\\n      atr_multiplier: 1.2\\n    - type: TREND\\n      method: EMA_ALIGNMENT\\n      ema_fast: 20\\n      ema_slow: 50\\n      require_both: true\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 3\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 30\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 12.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 22.0\\n\\nhedge:\\n  HedgeMode: PARTIAL\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: TREND_REVERSAL\\n      conditions: [EMA_CROSS, HALFTREND_FLIP]\\n      require_all: false\\n    - type: EMERGENCY_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 15.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxHedgeLayers: 2\\n    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.7\\n    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 4.0\\n    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600\\n    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 14400\\n    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 28.0\\n```\\n\\n## Template 2: HalfTrend EA\\n\\n```yaml\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: true\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: TREND\\n      method: HALFTREND_VALID\\n    - type: CANDLE\\n      method: PIN_BAR\\n      require_both: false\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 2\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 25\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 600\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 7200\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 12.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 22.0\\n\\nhedge:\\n  HedgeMode: TREND\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: TREND_REVERSAL\\n      conditions: [HALFTREND_FLIP]\\n    - type: EMERGENCY_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 15.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxHedgeLayers: 1\\n    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.8\\n    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 3.0\\n    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 900\\n    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 28800\\n    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 28.0\\n```\\n\\n## Template 3: SMC EA\\n\\n```yaml\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: true\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: STRUCTURE\\n      method: HIGHER_LOW\\n    - type: DISTANCE\\n      method: ATR_MULTIPLIER\\n      atr_period: 14\\n      atr_multiplier: 1.5\\n    - logic: AND\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 2\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 40\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 900\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 7200\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 10.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 20.0\\n\\nhedge:\\n  HedgeMode: TREND\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: STRUCTURE\\n      method: CHOCH_CONFIRMED\\n    - type: EMERGENCY_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 12.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxHedgeLayers: 2\\n    MaxHedgeRatio: 1.0\\n    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 4.0\\n    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600\\n    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 21600\\n    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 25.0\\n```\\n\\n## Template 4: Grid EA\\n\\n```yaml\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: true\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: CONTROLLED\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: DISTANCE\\n      method: FIXED_POINTS\\n      fixed_points: 50\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 5\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.0\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 50\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 0\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: false\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 3\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 20.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 35.0\\n\\nhedge:\\n  HedgeMode: EMERGENCY\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: EMERGENCY_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 20.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxHedgeLayers: 1\\n    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.5\\n    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 5.0\\n    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 1800\\n    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 43200\\n    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 40.0\\n```\\n\\n## Template 5: Mean Reversion EA\\n\\n```yaml\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: false\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: CONTROLLED\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: VOLATILITY\\n      method: ATR_CONTRACTION\\n    - type: DD\\n      method: FLOATING_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 5.0\\n    - logic: OR\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 3\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 20\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 3\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 10.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 18.0\\n\\n# hedge: disabled \\u2014 HedgeEnabled: false\\n# All hedge parameters are ignored by the engine\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 11 \\u2014 EA Platform Integration\\n\\n## Integration Contracts\\n\\n### Signal Engine to Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine\\n\\n| Event | Payload |\\n|---|---|\\n| SIGNAL_GENERATED | symbol, direction, strength, trigger_type |\\n| SIGNAL_INVALIDATED | symbol, reason \\u2014 may trigger Trend Recovery re-evaluation |\\n| TREND_SCORE_UPDATE | symbol, score, direction \\u2014 used by Trend Recovery triggers |\\n\\n### Entry Engine to Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine\\n\\n| Event | Payload |\\n|---|---|\\n| POSITION_OPENED | ticket, symbol, direction, lot, entry_price, chain_id |\\n| ENTRY_BLOCKED | reason \\u2014 R&amp;H state caused block \\u2014 logged |\\n\\n### Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine to Entry Engine\\n\\n| Event | Payload |\\n|---|---|\\n| BLOCK_NEW_SIGNALS | reason: RECOVERY_CHAIN_ACTIVE |\\n| RECOVERY_ORDER_REQUEST | symbol, direction, lot, type, chain_id, step |\\n| HEDGE_ORDER_REQUEST | symbol, direction, lot, mode, layer, hedge_id |\\n\\n### Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine to Exit Engine\\n\\n| Event | Payload |\\n|---|---|\\n| BASKET_EXIT_REQUEST | chain_id, tickets[], reason: BREAK_EVEN_HIT |\\n| HEDGE_UNWIND_REQUEST | hedge_id, tickets[], reason: CONDITION_CLEARED or TIMEOUT |\\n| EMERGENCY_FLATTEN | reason, dd_pct \\u2014 closes all |\\n\\n### Basket Engine to Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine\\n\\n| Data | Usage |\\n|---|---|\\n| BasketBreakEven | Break-even price for basket exit target |\\n| NetExposureLots | Used for MaxHedgeExposure validation |\\n| BasketFloatingPL | DD calculation inputs |\\n| ChainPositions[] | Recovery step count, lot distribution |\\n\\n## Logging Standard\\n\\nEvery engine action is logged in this format:\\n\\n`[TIMESTAMP] [ENGINE] [EVENT_TYPE] [SYMBOL] [DETAIL]`\\n\\nExamples:\\n\\n- 2026.06.06 14:00:01  RECOVERY  STEP_PLACED     EURUSD  Step=2, Lot=0.023, Entry=1.08420, Chain=C001\\n- 2026.06.06 14:05:22  SAFETY    BLOCKED         EURUSD  Rule=MaxRecoverySteps, Value=3, Limit=3\\n- 2026.06.06 14:15:44  HEDGE     EMERGENCY_PLACED EURUSD  Mode=EMERGENCY, Lot=0.10, DD=15.3%\\n- 2026.06.06 14:22:11  RISK      FLATTEN_ALL      ALL     DD=25.2%, Threshold=25.0%\\n- 2026.06.06 14:22:12  EXIT      FLATTEN_EXEC     EURUSD  Tickets=[1001,1002,1003], Reason=EMERGENCY\\n\\n## Portfolio Guardian Integration\\n\\nPortfolio Guardian monitors at the ACCOUNT level. It does not place trades. It does not modify positions. Its only output is signals and metrics.\\n\\nInputs: All open positions (symbol, lot, direction, PL), correlation matrix, account balance/equity/margin\\n\\nOutputs to Risk Engine: PortfolioDD_Pct, CorrelatedExposure_Pct, MarginUtilization_Pct\\n\\nOutputs to Hedge Engine: PORTFOLIO_HEDGE_SIGNAL when correlation or DD thresholds are breached\\n\\nAuthority: Portfolio Guardian reports TO Risk Engine. It is an observer and reporter \\u2014 not an executor.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Appendix A \\u2014 Parameter Quick Reference\\n\\n## Recovery Parameters\\n\\n| Parameter | Type | Default | Range | Description |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| RecoveryEnabled | Bool | false | \\u2014 | Master gate |\\n| RecoveryMode | Enum | NONE | NONE/SOFT/ADAPTIVE/CONTROLLED | Operating mode |\\n| MaxRecoverySteps | Int | 3 | 1\\u20135 | Max recovery orders per chain |\\n| RecoveryMultiplier | Float | 1.5 | 1.0\\u20132.0 | Lot multiplier per step |\\n| RecoveryDistance_Min_Points | Int | 20 | 10\\u2013100 | Min distance between entries |\\n| RecoveryCooldown_Seconds | Int | 300 | 60\\u20131800 | Min time between steps |\\n| OneRecoveryChainAtATime | Bool | true | \\u2014 | Portfolio chain limit |\\n| BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count | Int | 2 | 1\\u20135 | Loss count to trigger block |\\n| BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds | Int | 3600 | 1800\\u201386400 | Block duration |\\n| StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct | Float | 15.0 | 5\\u201325 | DD that suspends recovery |\\n| CloseAllAtDD_Pct | Float | 25.0 | 15\\u201340 | DD that closes all positions |\\n\\n## Hedge Parameters\\n\\n| Parameter | Type | Default | Range | Description |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| HedgeEnabled | Bool | false | \\u2014 | Master gate |\\n| HedgeMode | Enum | OFF | OFF/EMERGENCY/PARTIAL/BREAKOUT/TREND/RECOVERY | Operating mode |\\n| MaxHedgeLayers | Int | 2 | 1\\u20133 | Max simultaneous hedge positions |\\n| MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier | Float | 5.0 | 1.0\\u201310.0 | Max hedge lots = Base x mult |\\n| MaxHedgeRatio | Float | 1.0 | 0.3\\u20131.0 | Max hedge as fraction of exposure |\\n| HedgeCooldown_Seconds | Int | 600 | 300\\u20133600 | Min time between hedge orders |\\n| HedgeTimeout_Seconds | Int | 14400 | 3600\\u201386400 | Auto-unwind timeout |\\n| EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct | Float | 30.0 | 20\\u201350 | DD that closes all (incl. hedges) |\\n| NoRecursiveHedge | Bool | true | enforced | Cannot hedge a hedge position |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Appendix B \\u2014 Error Codes\\n\\n| Code | Engine | Meaning | Action |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| REC_001 | Recovery | MaxRecoverySteps exceeded | Hold position. Log. |\\n| REC_002 | Recovery | RecoveryMultiplier would exceed MaxLotSize | Reduce lot to cap or block. |\\n| REC_003 | Recovery | RecoveryCooldown active | Queue. Retry after cooldown. |\\n| REC_004 | Recovery | OneRecoveryChainAtATime \\u2014 chain already active | Block. Log. |\\n| REC_005 | Recovery | BlockRecoveryAfterLoss active | Block. Log. Resume after cooldown. |\\n| REC_006 | Recovery | StopRecoveryAtDD threshold breached | Suspend all recovery. |\\n| REC_007 | Recovery | Hedge engine suspended recovery | Log. Resume on HEDGE_RESOLVED. |\\n| HDG_001 | Hedge | MaxHedgeLayers exceeded | Block new hedge. |\\n| HDG_002 | Hedge | MaxHedgeRatio exceeded | Reduce lot to ratio cap. |\\n| HDG_003 | Hedge | HedgeCooldown active | Queue. Retry after cooldown. |\\n| HDG_004 | Hedge | NoRecursiveHedge \\u2014 target is hedge position | Block. Log error. |\\n| HDG_005 | Hedge | HedgeTimeout expired | Auto-unwind hedge position. |\\n| HDG_006 | Hedge | Broker does not support hedging | Startup halt. |\\n| RISK_001 | Risk | CloseAllAtDD triggered | Emergency flatten all. Halt EA. |\\n| RISK_002 | Risk | EmergencyFlatten triggered | Emergency flatten all. Halt EA. |\\n| CFG_001 | Config | CloseAllAtDD &lt;= StopRecoveryAtDD | Config error. Startup halt. |\\n| CFG_002 | Config | EmergencyFlatten &lt;= EmergencyHedge threshold | Config warning. |\\n| CFG_003 | Config | HedgeEnabled but broker netting mode | Startup halt. |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Appendix C \\u2014 Glossary\\n\\n| Term | Definition |\\n|---|---|\\n| Recovery Chain | A group of positions (original + recovery entries) managed together as one basket |\\n| Chain ID | Unique identifier assigned to a recovery chain (C001, C002...) |\\n| Break-Even | Weighted average entry price of all positions in the basket |\\n| BaseLot | The original lot size placed by the Signal Engine |\\n| Recovery Step | Each additional recovery order added after the original entry (Step 1, 2, 3...) |\\n| Hedge Layer | One hedge position (multiple layers = multiple hedge positions) |\\n| Hedge Ratio | HedgeLot / NetExposureLot \\u2014 fraction of exposure neutralized |\\n| Net Exposure | Sum of all BUY lots minus sum of all SELL lots for a symbol |\\n| Feature Gate | A boolean parameter that enables or disables an entire engine module |\\n| Authority | The rank of an engine in the hierarchy that determines who can override whom |\\n| Recursive Hedge | A hedge placed on a position that is already a hedge \\u2014 prohibited |\\n| Basket Exit | Closing all positions in a recovery chain simultaneously at break-even |\\n| DD | Drawdown \\u2014 expressed as floating loss / account balance x 100 |\\n| ATR | Average True Range \\u2014 volatility measure used for dynamic distance calculations |\\n| BOS | Break of Structure \\u2014 price breaks a prior swing high or low |\\n| CHoCH | Change of Character \\u2014 structure switches from bullish to bearish or vice versa |\\n| HL | Higher Low \\u2014 bullish structure: each pullback ends above the previous low |\\n| LH | Lower High \\u2014 bearish structure: each rally ends below the previous high |\&quot;, \&quot;confidentiality_level\&quot;: \&quot;internal\&quot;}&quot;" result="&quot;\n\n---\n### ✅ Ready\n📁 ## 🔍 SCRUTINY REPORT — `recovery-and-hedge-engine.md` v1.0.0

กำลัง audit แบบ senior architect ครับ ตรวจ
