สรุป: **4 High / 5 Medium / 3 Low** — generate v1.1.0 พร้อม fixes ทั้งหมดเลยครับ
<details type="tool_calls" done="true" id="toolu_012bVD353LZnGRABxhDw7d5v" name="SCGC-doc-gen-mcp_generate_word" arguments="&quot;{\&quot;title\&quot;: \&quot;recovery-and-hedge-engine\&quot;, \&quot;category_label\&quot;: \&quot;EA LAB \\u00b7 PLATFORM SKILL \\u00b7 v1.1.0\&quot;, \&quot;subtitle\&quot;: \&quot;Universal Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine \\u2014 Strategy-Agnostic Specification\&quot;, \&quot;date_label\&quot;: \&quot;June 2026 \\u00b7 EA Lab Architecture Team\&quot;, \&quot;author\&quot;: \&quot;EA Lab Architecture Team | June 2026\&quot;, \&quot;content\&quot;: \&quot;# Changelog\\n\\n| Version | Date | Changes |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| 1.0.0 | 2026-06-30 | Initial release |\\n| 1.1.0 | 2026-06-30 | Added: Mode Decision Tree, Trigger Logic Combiner, CloseAllAtDD constraint enforcement, RECOVERY mode exit authority, Breakout strength scoring reference, BASKET_BE_UPDATED feedback event, HALT definition, Grid cooldown guard, Appendix B error code reconciliation, ASCII flow diagrams |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Overview\\n\\n**Version:** 1.1.0 | **Status:** Production-Ready | **Scope:** Universal \\u2014 Strategy-Agnostic\\n\\nThis skill defines the canonical Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine for the EA Lab platform. It is reusable across all EA strategies: Trend Following, HalfTrend, Breakout, SMC, Mean Reversion, Grid, and Hybrid systems. No section of this specification assumes any particular signal logic.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 1 \\u2014 Design Philosophy\\n\\n## Recovery Engine vs Hedge Engine\\n\\nThese are two fundamentally different systems. They must never be conflated, chained implicitly, or assumed to co-activate.\\n\\n**Recovery Engine**\\n\\n- Objective: Improve average entry price\\n- Mechanism: Add positions in the SAME direction as the loss\\n- Outcome: Reduce break-even distance and coordinate basket exits\\n- Risk profile: Increases total exposure\\n\\n&gt; \\\&quot;I am losing. I will improve my position to escape faster.\\\&quot;\\n\\n**Hedge Engine**\\n\\n- Objective: Reduce portfolio exposure\\n- Mechanism: Add positions in the OPPOSITE direction\\n- Outcome: Reduce drawdown and survive adverse movement\\n- Risk profile: Locks in spread cost and opportunity cost\\n\\n&gt; \\\&quot;I am losing. I will neutralize exposure to survive this move.\\\&quot;\\n\\n## Independence Rules\\n\\n| Rule | Recovery Engine | Hedge Engine |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| May activate without the other | Yes | Yes |\\n| May trigger the other | Never | Never |\\n| Assumes the other is active | Never | Never |\\n| May block the other | Yes \\u2014 via Authority Model | Yes \\u2014 via Authority Model |\\n| Shares position sizing | No \\u2014 Independent | No \\u2014 Independent |\\n| Shares exit logic | No \\u2014 Independent | No \\u2014 Independent |\\n\\n## Strategy Compatibility Matrix\\n\\n| EA Strategy | Recovery | Hedge | Notes |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| Trend Following | Yes | Yes | Recovery follows trend direction |\\n| HalfTrend | Yes | Yes | Recovery on HalfTrend confirmation only |\\n| Breakout | Yes | Yes | Hedge on false breakout confirmation |\\n| SMC | Yes | Yes | Recovery at BOS/CHoCH structure levels |\\n| Mean Reversion | Yes | Caution | Hedge conflicts with MR core philosophy |\\n| Grid | Yes | Caution | Grid is inherently recovery \\u2014 hedge rarely needed |\\n| Hybrid | Yes | Yes | Requires careful authority configuration |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 2 \\u2014 Recovery Engine\\n\\n## System Architecture\\n\\n```\\n[Recovery Trigger Monitor]\\n        |\\n        v\\n[Safety Rule Gauntlet] --BLOCK--&gt; [Log + Cooldown]\\n        |\\n        v PASS\\n[Recovery Mode Selector]\\n  |-- NONE      -&gt; No action, pass-through\\n  |-- SOFT      -&gt; Fixed distance, fixed lot\\n  |-- ADAPTIVE  -&gt; ATR-based distance, scaled lot\\n  `-- CONTROLLED-&gt; Capped steps, multiplier-limited\\n        |\\n        v\\n[Recovery Order Manager]\\n  |-- Entry: Market or Pending\\n  |-- Lot: BaseLot x RecoveryMultiplier^step\\n  `-- Comment tag: [EA_ID][RECOVERY][Step_N]\\n        |\\n        v\\n[Basket Break-Even Calculator]\\n  `-- Recalculate weighted average entry\\n  `-- Emit BASKET_BE_UPDATED to Exit Engine\\n```\\n\\n## Mode Selection Decision Tree\\n\\nUse this tree before configuring RecoveryMode for any EA:\\n\\n1. Is recovery needed at all?\\n   - No: set RecoveryMode = NONE. Stop here.\\n   - Yes: continue to step 2.\\n\\n2. Is the strategy expecting only brief, single retracements against a confirmed trend?\\n   - Yes: set RecoveryMode = SOFT. Stop here.\\n   - No: continue to step 3.\\n\\n3. Does the instrument or timeframe produce variable volatility requiring dynamic distance calibration?\\n   - Yes: set RecoveryMode = ADAPTIVE. Stop here.\\n   - No: continue to step 4.\\n\\n4. Is the strategy designed around a known range, mean reversion, or grid logic where multiple recovery layers are a structural feature?\\n   - Yes: set RecoveryMode = CONTROLLED.\\n   - No: default to ADAPTIVE as the safer general choice.\\n\\n## Recovery Modes\\n\\n### MODE: NONE\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Objective | Disable recovery entirely. EA operates single-position only. |\\n| Risk Profile | Baseline \\u2014 no additional exposure added |\\n| Intended Use | Conservative EAs, during optimization, or when capital is constrained |\\n| Advantages | Cleanest risk profile. No compounding exposure. Backtest results are most predictable. Optimizer can properly isolate signal quality. |\\n| Failure Modes | A losing position has no recovery path. Single trade losses directly impact account equity. May cause unnecessary stops on valid setups. |\\n\\n### MODE: SOFT\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Objective | Place a single recovery order at a fixed distance. Simple and predictable. |\\n| Risk Profile | Low-to-Moderate. Maximum 1 additional order per chain. |\\n| Intended Use | Trend Following, HalfTrend, Breakout EAs where brief retracements are common |\\n| Advantages | Minimal complexity. Easy to backtest. Break-even distance cut by approximately 50% on successful recovery. Spreads cost over 2 orders only. |\\n| Failure Modes | Only one recovery attempt. If price continues beyond recovery entry, exposure doubles with no further mitigation. Does not scale with volatility. |\\n\\n*Example flow:* Original Entry BUY at 1.3000 \\u2192 price moves -50 pts \\u2192 Recovery BUY at 1.2950 (fixed distance = 50 pts) \\u2192 Basket Break-Even = 1.2975 \\u2192 Exit at 1.2985 (BE + profit buffer)\\n\\n### MODE: ADAPTIVE\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Objective | Place recovery orders at dynamic distances based on market volatility (ATR). Scale lot size proportionally to maintain consistent break-even distance. |\\n| Risk Profile | Moderate. Exposure scales with step count but remains volatility-calibrated. |\\n| Intended Use | SMC, HalfTrend, Hybrid EAs operating across variable-volatility instruments or timeframes |\\n| Advantages | Self-adjusting to market conditions. Avoids premature recovery in high-volatility environments. Break-even always proportionally achievable. Works well in trending markets. |\\n| Failure Modes | ATR can expand dramatically in news events \\u2014 recovery distance can become unreachable. Complexity makes manual intervention harder. Optimization surface is larger. |\\n\\nCalculation formulas:\\n- Recovery Distance = ATR(Period) x ATR_Multiplier\\n- Recovery Lot = BaseLot x RecoveryMultiplier^step\\n- Break-Even = Sum(Lot_i x Entry_i) / Sum(Lot_i)\\n\\n### MODE: CONTROLLED\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Objective | Full recovery sequence with hard caps on steps and multiplier ceiling. Structured martingale with enforced limits. |\\n| Risk Profile | Moderate-to-High. Maximum exposure is deterministic and pre-calculated before the first trade. |\\n| Intended Use | Grid EAs, Mean Reversion EAs, Hybrid systems where recovery sequences are core to the strategy design |\\n| Advantages | Maximum exposure is known before trade entry. Break-even analysis is deterministic. Safety caps prevent runaway exposure. Suitable for mean-reversion asset classes. |\\n| Failure Modes | Maximum steps can still produce significant drawdown if market trends strongly. Must not be used on strong-trending assets without a hard stop condition. A full chain failure produces the largest single loss of all modes. |\\n\\nPre-calculation table (BaseLot = 0.01, MaxLotSize = 0.10):\\n\\n| Step | Multiplier 1.0x | Multiplier 1.5x | Multiplier 2.0x |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| 0 (original) | 0.01 | 0.01 | 0.01 |\\n| 1 | 0.01 | 0.015 | 0.02 |\\n| 2 | 0.01 | 0.023 | 0.04 |\\n| 3 | 0.01 | 0.034 | 0.08 |\\n| 4 | 0.01 | 0.051 | 0.16 \\u2014 BLOCKED by MaxLotSize |\\n\\n&gt; Always run this table before deployment. If any step exceeds MaxLotSize, reduce RecoveryMultiplier or MaxRecoverySteps until no row is blocked.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 3 \\u2014 Recovery Triggers\\n\\n## Trigger Architecture and Logic Combiner\\n\\nEach trigger type is an independent module. EAs enable exactly the triggers relevant to their signal logic.\\n\\n```\\n[Distance]  [Candle]  [Trend]  [Structure]  [DD]  [Volatility]\\n     |           |        |          |         |         |\\n     `-----+-----&#x27;--------+-----------+---------+---------&#x27;\\n           |\\n    [Logic Combiner]\\n      |-- AND  : ALL enabled triggers must fire simultaneously\\n      |-- OR   : ANY single enabled trigger is sufficient to fire\\n      `-- WEIGHTED: assign score per trigger, fire when sum &gt;= threshold\\n           |\\n     ,-----+-------.\\n     |              |\\nTRIGGER FIRED   TRIGGER BLOCKED\\n     |              |\\n[Safety Gauntlet] [Log Rejection + reason code]\\n```\\n\\n**Logic Combiner configuration (per EA):**\\n\\n| Logic Mode | When to use | Risk |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| OR | Fast-response strategies; any signal is sufficient | Higher false-trigger rate |\\n| AND | High-conviction strategies requiring full confluence | May miss valid entries |\\n| WEIGHTED | Hybrid strategies where some triggers carry more conviction | Requires calibration |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 1: Distance Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Formula | Default |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| Fixed Points | CurrentPrice - EntryPrice &gt; FixedPoints | 50 pts |\\n| ATR-Based | CurrentPrice - EntryPrice &gt; ATR(14) | 1.0x ATR(14) |\\n| ATR Multiplier | CurrentPrice - EntryPrice &gt; ATR(14) x Multiplier | 1.5x ATR(14) |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Simple, deterministic, easy to backtest. No indicator dependency. Fires reliably regardless of market structure. |\\n| Weaknesses | Ignores market structure entirely. May fire during normal retracements of a valid trend. Fixed distance becomes irrelevant across instruments with different volatility profiles. |\\n| Best Use Cases | SOFT mode on Trend Following and HalfTrend EAs. Grid EAs where fixed levels are a structural feature. Low-volatility instruments (stable FX pairs). |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 2: Candle Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| Engulfing | Prior candle body fully engulfed by current candle body in recovery direction |\\n| Pin Bar | Shadow length &gt;= 2x body length; close &gt;= 60% of candle range in recovery direction |\\n| Strong Close | Candle closes in top or bottom 25% of range AND candle range &gt;= 1.0x ATR(14) |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Adds price action confluence to recovery decision. Reduces entries at arbitrary distance points. Aligns with signal logic already present in many EA frameworks. |\\n| Weaknesses | Prone to false signals in low-volatility, choppy markets. May delay recovery beyond optimal entry point. Sensitive to broker feed quality and candle formation timing. |\\n| Best Use Cases | SMC EAs, HalfTrend EAs. ADAPTIVE mode. Higher timeframes (H1, H4) where candle formations are more structurally significant. |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 3: Trend Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| HalfTrend Valid | HalfTrend indicator arrow/color still points in original trade direction \\u2014 no flip detected |\\n| EMA Alignment | EMA(fast) still above EMA(slow) for BUY positions; below for SELL positions |\\n| Trend Score | Internal trend score &gt;= defined threshold (recommended: &gt;= 60 out of 100) |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Highest quality recovery entries of all trigger types. Ensures recovery only occurs in a directionally valid context. Prevents recovery entries into a confirmed full trend reversal. |\\n| Weaknesses | If the trend invalidates just before the recovery threshold is reached, recovery is blocked and the position may be left unmanaged. Lagging indicators may confirm trend validity too late in fast-moving conditions. |\\n| Best Use Cases | Trend Following EAs, HalfTrend EAs. ADAPTIVE and CONTROLLED modes where recovery quality matters more than recovery speed. Medium-to-long timeframes (H4, D1). |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 4: Structure Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| BOS | Price breaks the most recent significant swing high (for BUY) or swing low (for SELL) in the recovery direction |\\n| CHoCH | Pattern shifts: Higher-High to Higher-Low or Lower-High to Lower-Low confirmed in recovery direction |\\n| Higher Low (HL) | For BUY recovery: price forms a confirmed swing low above the previous swing low |\\n| Lower High (LH) | For SELL recovery: price forms a confirmed swing high below the previous swing high |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Institutionally validated entry levels aligned with Smart Money logic. Recovery entries align with market participants likely to push price in the recovery direction. Highest structural quality. |\\n| Weaknesses | Structure breaks can be false \\u2014 stop hunts and liquidity grabs are common. Structure identification requires additional computation and can be subjective across different algorithms. Fewer triggers generated in low-structure environments. |\\n| Best Use Cases | SMC EAs, Breakout EAs. ADAPTIVE mode where structural confluence is essential. H1\\u2013H4 timeframes where structure is cleaner and more reliable. |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 5: DD Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| Floating DD % | FloatingLoss / AccountBalance &gt; Threshold_Pct |\\n| Cycle DD % | CycleLoss / CycleStartBalance &gt; Threshold_Pct |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | Directly tied to account financial impact rather than arbitrary price distances. Prevents recovery from triggering on minor price noise. Ensures recovery is proportional to actual financial stress level. |\\n| Weaknesses | Delayed trigger relative to price-based methods. In fast-moving markets, DD may exceed recovery threshold before any recovery order can be executed. Can create urgency-based decisions that override signal quality considerations. |\\n| Best Use Cases | CONTROLLED mode. Grid and Mean Reversion EAs where DD thresholds are pre-defined as first-class strategy parameters and position sizing is calibrated around them. |\\n\\n## Trigger Type 6: Volatility Recovery\\n\\n| Variant | Condition |\\n|---|---|\\n| ATR Expansion | ATR(current_period) &gt; ATR(reference_period) x Expansion_Multiplier |\\n| ATR Contraction | ATR(current_period) &lt; ATR(reference_period) x Contraction_Multiplier |\\n\\n| Attribute | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Strengths | ATR Expansion recovery: targets high-momentum moves where trend continuation probability is elevated. ATR Contraction recovery: targets consolidation phases where mean reversion is most statistically predictable. |\\n| Weaknesses | ATR is a lagging indicator. Expansion can signal a trend already at exhaustion. Contraction signals can be followed by further contraction with no imminent reversal. |\\n| Best Use Cases | ATR Expansion: Breakout EAs, Trend Following EAs. ATR Contraction: Mean Reversion EAs, Grid EAs. Both variants: Hybrid systems designed to adapt to volatility regime. |\\n\\n## Trigger Comparison Matrix\\n\\n| Trigger | Speed | Noise Resistance | Structure Awareness | Best Mode |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| Distance | Fast | Low | None | SOFT, CONTROLLED |\\n| Candle | Medium | Medium | Medium | ADAPTIVE |\\n| Trend | Medium | High | Low | ADAPTIVE |\\n| Structure | Slow | High | High | ADAPTIVE |\\n| DD | Slow | High | None | CONTROLLED |\\n| Volatility | Medium | Medium | None | ADAPTIVE, CONTROLLED |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 4 \\u2014 Recovery Safety Rules\\n\\nAll safety rules are evaluated in sequence BEFORE any recovery order is submitted. A single rule violation blocks the entire recovery action and logs the rejection reason.\\n\\n```\\nRecovery Request Received\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 1: MaxRecoverySteps ---------&gt; BLOCK if steps at ceiling\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 2: RecoveryMultiplier -------&gt; BLOCK if lot exceeds MaxLotSize\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 3: RecoveryDistance ---------&gt; BLOCK if separation below minimum\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 4: RecoveryCooldown ---------&gt; BLOCK if within cooldown window\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 5: OneRecoveryChainAtATime --&gt; BLOCK if chain already active\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 6: BlockRecoveryAfterLoss --&gt; BLOCK if consecutive loss limit hit\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 7: StopRecoveryAtDD ---------&gt; BLOCK if account DD exceeded\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 8: CloseAllAtDD -------------&gt; EMERGENCY FLATTEN ALL\\n         |\\n         v ALL PASS\\nRecovery Order Submitted\\n```\\n\\n## Rule 1: MaxRecoverySteps\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer |\\n| Default | 3 |\\n| Recommended Range | 1 \\u2013 5 |\\n| Function | Hard ceiling on the number of recovery orders in a single chain. Step 0 = original entry. Steps 1 through N = recovery entries. When ceiling is reached, no further recovery orders are placed. |\\n| On Violation | Recovery blocked. Position held open. Basket managed by Exit Engine only until the chain resolves. |\\n| Warning | Setting above 5 produces exponential lot growth under any multiplier above 1.0. Never exceed unless the strategy is explicitly designed for it and maximum exposure has been pre-calculated and accepted. |\\n\\n## Rule 2: RecoveryMultiplier\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float |\\n| Default | 1.5 |\\n| Recommended Range | 1.0 \\u2013 2.0 |\\n| Function | Lot size multiplier applied at each recovery step. Formula: RecoveryLot_N = BaseLot x RecoveryMultiplier^N |\\n| On Violation | If the computed lot for the next step would exceed MaxLotSize, the recovery order is blocked entirely. No partial-lot reduction is permitted \\u2014 block only. |\\n| Warning | Multiplier of 2.0 over 3 steps produces 8x the base lot. Always pre-calculate the full step table before deployment. |\\n\\n## Rule 3: RecoveryDistance\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (points) |\\n| Default | 20 pts (5-digit brokers) |\\n| Recommended Range | 10 \\u2013 100 pts depending on instrument ATR |\\n| Function | Minimum distance in points between the most recent recovery entry and the proposed new recovery entry. Prevents order stacking too close together. |\\n| On Violation | Recovery order is deferred. The engine re-evaluates every tick until price reaches the minimum separation distance. |\\n| Calibration Note | Set to at least 0.5x ATR(14) of the traded instrument. Too small produces excessive spread cost. Too large makes recovery unreachable within a normal session. |\\n\\n## Rule 4: RecoveryCooldown\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (seconds) |\\n| Default | 300 seconds |\\n| Recommended Range | 60 \\u2013 1800 seconds |\\n| Function | Minimum elapsed time between consecutive recovery entries within the same chain. Measured from the timestamp of the previous recovery order fill. |\\n| On Violation | Recovery request is queued internally. Re-evaluated after cooldown window expires. |\\n| Rationale | Prevents multiple recovery orders from stacking during rapid price movement such as a news spike. Ensures each recovery entry has time to establish before the next is considered. |\\n\\n## Rule 5: OneRecoveryChainAtATime\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Boolean |\\n| Default | true |\\n| Function | When true: if any recovery chain is active on any symbol, no new recovery chain may be initiated on any other symbol until the active chain fully resolves (closed at break-even, closed at DD limit, or manually closed). |\\n| On Violation | New recovery requests from other symbols are queued or blocked until the active chain clears. |\\n| Rationale | Prevents compounding exposure across multiple simultaneous recovery chains. Provides portfolio-level risk control beyond per-position rules. |\\n| Note | For Grid EAs that intentionally run multiple parallel chains, set this to false. Ensure MaxRecoverySteps and lot sizes are calibrated accordingly. |\\n\\n## Rule 6: BlockRecoveryAfterLoss\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (consecutive loss count) |\\n| Default | 2 consecutive chain losses |\\n| Cooldown Parameter | BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds = 3600 |\\n| Function | After N consecutive recovery chains that closed at a loss (not at break-even or profit), the recovery module enters a cooling state. All recovery requests are rejected until the cooldown timer expires. |\\n| On Violation | Recovery module logs RECOVERY_COOLING state. All requests return REC_005. Cooldown begins from the timestamp of the Nth loss close. |\\n| Rationale | Consecutive chain losses signal that the current market regime is incompatible with the configured recovery parameters. This rule prevents continued exposure amplification into a persistently adverse condition. |\\n\\n## Rule 7: StopRecoveryAtDD\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float (percentage of account balance) |\\n| Default | 15.0% |\\n| Function | If account floating drawdown exceeds this threshold at the moment of recovery evaluation, all recovery activity is suspended immediately. No new recovery orders are placed. |\\n| On Violation | Recovery module enters SUSPENDED state. Basket is held open. Exit Engine takes full control. Hedge Engine may activate independently per its own authority. |\\n| Rationale | At 15% floating DD, the strategic priority shifts entirely to capital preservation. Adding more exposure through recovery at this point is counterproductive. |\\n\\n## Rule 8: CloseAllAtDD\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float (percentage of account balance) |\\n| Default | 25.0% |\\n| Hard Constraint | CloseAllAtDD_Pct MUST be greater than StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct. The startup validator enforces this. If CloseAllAtDD_Pct &lt;= StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct, the EA will not initialize (returns CFG_001 and halts). |\\n| Function | If account floating drawdown reaches this hard threshold, ALL open positions are closed immediately at market \\u2014 including all recovery chain positions and all active hedge positions. |\\n| On Trigger | Emergency flatten executed. EA transitions to HALTED state. No new orders permitted until operator performs a manual reset and restarts. |\\n| Rationale | This is the absolute last line of defense. It guarantees the account is never exposed to a margin call due to unmanaged recovery or hedge positions. The HALTED state requires human review before trading resumes. |\\n\\n## Default Configuration Summary\\n\\n```yaml\\nrecovery_safety:\\n  MaxRecoverySteps: 3\\n  RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n  RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 20\\n  RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300\\n  OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n  BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2\\n  BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600\\n  StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 15.0\\n  CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 25.0\\n  # CONSTRAINT: CloseAllAtDD_Pct must always exceed StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct\\n  # Violation of this constraint triggers CFG_001 at startup and halts the EA\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 5 \\u2014 Hedge Engine\\n\\n## System Architecture\\n\\n```\\n[Hedge Trigger Monitor]\\n        |\\n        v\\n[Safety Rule Gauntlet] --BLOCK--&gt; [Log + Cooldown]\\n        |\\n        v PASS\\n[Hedge Mode Selector]\\n  |-- OFF        -&gt; No action\\n  |-- EMERGENCY  -&gt; Full hedge, immediate, no signal required\\n  |-- PARTIAL    -&gt; Defined ratio hedge\\n  |-- BREAKOUT   -&gt; Counter-directional on confirmed breakout\\n  |-- TREND      -&gt; Hedge against detected trend reversal\\n  `-- RECOVERY   -&gt; Bounded hedge while recovery chain is active\\n        |\\n        v\\n[Hedge Order Manager]\\n  |-- HedgeLot = NetExposureLot x HedgeRatio\\n  |-- Direction: opposite of net basket direction\\n  `-- Tag: [EA_ID][HEDGE][Mode][Layer_N]\\n        |\\n        v\\n[Hedge Unwind Monitor]\\n  |-- Condition cleared -&gt; emit HEDGE_UNWIND_REQUEST to Exit Engine\\n  `-- Timeout exceeded  -&gt; emit HEDGE_TIMEOUT_UNWIND to Exit Engine\\n```\\n\\n## Hedge Modes\\n\\n### MODE: OFF\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Hedge engine is entirely inactive. No hedge positions are opened under any condition, including emergency drawdown scenarios. |\\n| When to Use | Conservative strategies, single-currency EAs, brokers in netting mode, or when the operator has confirmed hedging is not appropriate for the account. |\\n| Effect | All hedge triggers return immediately without evaluation. HedgeEnabled=false enforces this mode regardless of HedgeMode setting. |\\n\\n### MODE: EMERGENCY\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Activated when account DD exceeds EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct. Places a hedge equal to or near the full net open exposure immediately. No signal confirmation is required \\u2014 speed is the priority. |\\n| Trigger | DD-based. Fully automatic. Highest priority within the Hedge Engine. |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 1.0 (100% of net exposure \\u2014 full neutralization) |\\n| Exit Condition | Price recovery to within HedgeBreakEven_Buffer of the original basket break-even, OR HedgeTimeout expires, OR CloseAllAtDD is triggered. |\\n| Risk | Locks in the current loss level at the moment of hedge placement. Spread cost on unwind. If the market reverses immediately after hedging, the opportunity to exit at a better price is lost. |\\n| Rationale | Survival above all else. Stop the financial bleeding and create time to evaluate next steps without exposure risk. |\\n\\n### MODE: PARTIAL\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Activated by a moderate DD or exposure threshold that has not yet reached emergency level. Places a partial hedge covering a defined percentage of net exposure. |\\n| Trigger | Configurable DD threshold (lower than Emergency) or net lot exposure threshold |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.3 \\u2013 0.7 (configurable per strategy) |\\n| Exit Condition | Net exposure normalizes to below threshold, OR triggering condition fully clears |\\n| Risk | Does not fully protect the account \\u2014 adverse movement beyond the hedge ratio still produces net losses. May require escalation to Emergency mode. |\\n| Rationale | Reduce directional delta without committing to full neutralization. Retains upside potential if the original direction recovers. |\\n\\n### MODE: BREAKOUT\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Hedge placed in the direction of a confirmed breakout that is moving counter to the original trade direction. Designed to profit from or survive a false breakout that has now confirmed as a genuine directional move. |\\n| Trigger | BOS confirmation, range breakout with momentum validation, or ATR expansion in counter-direction |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 (scaled to breakout strength score \\u2014 see Trigger 3 in Section 6) |\\n| Exit Condition | Breakout fails and price reverts past entry level, OR hedge profit target reached on the breakout side |\\n| Risk | If the breakout reverses, the hedge position generates a loss that adds to the original position loss. Creates double jeopardy if the breakout signal is false. |\\n| Rationale | When a breakout against the position is confirmed by multiple conditions, a directional hedge reduces original exposure while participating in the new momentum. |\\n\\n### MODE: TREND\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | Hedge placed when trend analysis across multiple indicators signals a confirmed reversal against the open position. The hedge captures the emerging new trend direction. |\\n| Trigger | Trend reversal signals: EMA crossover, HalfTrend flip, trend score collapse \\u2014 see Trigger 4 in Section 6 |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 (configurable) |\\n| Exit Condition | Primary trend indicator returns to original direction, OR hedge profit target reached, OR HedgeTimeout expires |\\n| Risk | If the trend reversal indicator produces a false signal, both the original position and the hedge are simultaneously underwater. Combined spread cost on both sides. |\\n| Rationale | When trend evidence from multiple independent indicators is strong, the hedge reduces original directional exposure while capturing the new trend momentum. |\\n\\n### MODE: RECOVERY\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Description | A partial or full hedge placed specifically during an active recovery chain. The hedge neutralizes the additional risk exposure added by recovery orders while the chain continues its path toward break-even. |\\n| Trigger | Recovery chain active AND basket floating DD &gt; RecoveryHedgeDD_Pct |\\n| Hedge Ratio | Calculated as: HedgeLot = RecoveryExtraExposure x HedgeRatio_Recovery |\\n| Exit Authority | When the recovery chain closes (basket break-even hit or chain terminated), the Exit Engine is responsible for unwinding this hedge simultaneously with the basket close. The hedge MUST NOT outlive the recovery chain it was placed to protect. |\\n| Risk | The hedge in recovery direction partially offsets the break-even improvement that recovery orders provide. The net effect is a higher break-even target and a longer resolution time. Must be used carefully. |\\n| Rationale | Provides a bounded ceiling on recovery chain exposure. The recovery still proceeds toward break-even, but the maximum additional loss is mathematically bounded by the hedge ratio. |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 6 \\u2014 Hedge Triggers\\n\\n## Trigger 1: Emergency DD Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | FloatingDD_Pct &gt;= EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct (default: 15.0%) |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 1.0 \\u2014 full neutralization of net basket exposure |\\n| Expected Behavior | Immediate market order in the direction opposite to the net basket. Emits HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE to all engines. Recovery engine suspended immediately. |\\n| Risk | Position is locked at the current loss level. Spread cost accumulated on both sides. If market reverses immediately after hedge placement, the opportunity to improve is missed until the hedge is unwound. |\\n\\n## Trigger 2: Partial Exposure Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | NetExposureLots &gt;= MaxExposureLots_Threshold OR FloatingDD_Pct &gt;= PartialHedgeDD_Pct (default: 8.0%) |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.3 \\u2013 0.5 (configurable) |\\n| Expected Behavior | Partial hedge placed. Original positions continue to accumulate PL. Net directional delta is reduced but not eliminated. |\\n| Risk | Insufficient protection if adverse movement accelerates to Emergency threshold. May require escalation. |\\n\\n## Trigger 3: Breakout Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | Price breaks a confirmed structure level (BOS) against the original trade direction WITH momentum validation |\\n| Hedge Ratio | Scaled to Breakout Strength Score (see table below) |\\n| Expected Behavior | Hedge placed in the breakout direction at BOS confirmation. If original trade&#x27;s break-even level is reached, original basket is closed. Hedge is then managed independently as a new directional position. |\\n| Risk | Breakout may be a stop hunt or liquidity grab \\u2014 the hedge position immediately goes underwater. Spread costs on both sides. Requires strong breakout filter configuration. |\\n\\n**Breakout Strength Scoring:**\\n\\n| Condition Present | Score |\\n|---|---|\\n| BOS confirmed on primary timeframe | +30 |\\n| ATR expansion &gt;= 1.5x reference ATR | +25 |\\n| Volume spike &gt;= 1.5x average (if available) | +20 |\\n| Momentum candle close in top/bottom 10% of range | +15 |\\n| Higher timeframe structure alignment | +10 |\\n\\n| Total Score | Hedge Ratio Applied |\\n|---|---|\\n| &lt; 40 | No hedge \\u2014 insufficient confirmation |\\n| 40 \\u2013 59 | 0.5 (50% of net exposure) |\\n| 60 \\u2013 79 | 0.75 (75% of net exposure) |\\n| 80 \\u2013 100 | 1.0 (100% of net exposure) |\\n\\n## Trigger 4: Trend Reversal Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | Primary reversal signal: EMA_Cross_Confirmed AND HalfTrend_Flip AND TrendScore &lt; 40 |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 0.8 (configurable) |\\n| Expected Behavior | Hedge placed in the direction of the detected new trend. Original position is held open and monitored for a recovery opportunity. If the original position reaches break-even, it is closed and the hedge continues as an independent position. |\\n| Risk | Multiple indicator confirmation still produces false reversals in choppy, range-bound markets. Both positions simultaneously losing is the worst-case outcome. |\\n\\n## Trigger 5: Candle Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | Strong reversal candle against the open position direction: body engulfs prior candle, close in top or bottom 20% of range, AND candle range &gt;= 1.5x ATR(14) |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.3 \\u2013 0.5 |\\n| Expected Behavior | Quick partial hedge placed at candle close. Re-evaluated on the next completed candle. If reversal continues: escalate to Trend Reversal Hedge. If reversal fails and price recovers: unwind hedge immediately. |\\n| Risk | Single-candle signals have high false-positive rates, particularly on lower timeframes (M15 and below). Use primarily on H1 and above. |\\n\\n## Trigger 6: Structure Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | CHoCH confirmed (structural shift from bullish to bearish or vice versa) AND liquidity pool swept on the opposite side |\\n| Hedge Ratio | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 |\\n| Expected Behavior | Hedge placed at CHoCH confirmation candle close. Treated as high-conviction structural evidence. Held until original position can be exited at break-even or the defined maximum loss level. |\\n| Risk | CHoCH misidentification is common in complex market conditions. Requires a clean, liquid price action environment for reliable identification. |\\n\\n## Trigger 7: Portfolio Hedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Trigger Condition | Portfolio Guardian emits signal: PortfolioDD_Pct &gt;= PortfolioHedgeThreshold_Pct OR CorrelatedExposure_Pct &gt;= CorrelationThreshold_Pct |\\n| Hedge Ratio | Calculated at portfolio level \\u2014 proportional to the degree of correlated net exposure |\\n| Expected Behavior | Hedge placed on the most liquid correlated instrument available (or an index instrument if configured). Reduces systemic exposure across all correlated open positions simultaneously. |\\n| Risk | Correlation assumptions can break down during risk-off events \\u2014 precisely the moment when portfolio hedges are most critical. Over-reliance on correlation models in crisis conditions is a known failure mode. |\\n\\n## Hedge Trigger Comparison Matrix\\n\\n| Trigger | Speed | Signal Quality | Default Hedge Ratio | Best Mode |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| Emergency DD | Immediate | N/A (DD-driven) | 1.0 | EMERGENCY |\\n| Partial Exposure | Medium | High | 0.3 \\u2013 0.5 | PARTIAL |\\n| Breakout | Medium | Medium (score-gated) | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 | BREAKOUT |\\n| Trend Reversal | Slow | High | 0.5 \\u2013 0.8 | TREND |\\n| Candle | Fast | Low\\u2013Medium | 0.3 \\u2013 0.5 | PARTIAL |\\n| Structure | Slow | High | 0.5 \\u2013 1.0 | TREND, RECOVERY |\\n| Portfolio | Medium | High | Variable | PARTIAL, EMERGENCY |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 7 \\u2014 Hedge Safety Rules\\n\\nAll hedge safety rules are evaluated before any hedge order is placed. A single violation blocks the hedge action and logs the rejection with a reason code.\\n\\n```\\nHedge Request Received\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 1: MaxHedgeLayers ----------&gt; BLOCK if layer ceiling exceeded\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 2: MaxHedgeExposure ---------&gt; BLOCK or reduce lot to capacity\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 3: MaxHedgeRatio -----------&gt; BLOCK if ratio exceeds cap\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 4: HedgeCooldown -----------&gt; BLOCK if within cooldown window\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 5: HedgeTimeout (monitor) --&gt; AUTO-UNWIND if timeout exceeded\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 6: EmergencyFlatten --------&gt; CLOSE ALL if combined DD threshold hit\\n         |\\n         v\\nRule 7: NoRecursiveHedge --------&gt; BLOCK if target position is tagged HEDGE\\n         |\\n         v ALL PASS\\nHedge Order Submitted\\n```\\n\\n## Rule 1: MaxHedgeLayers\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer |\\n| Default | 2 |\\n| Recommended Range | 1 \\u2013 3 |\\n| Function | Maximum number of simultaneous hedge positions open at any one time, counted across all modes and all symbols. |\\n| On Violation | New hedge request is blocked. Existing hedge positions continue to be managed by the Hedge Unwind Monitor and Exit Engine. |\\n| Rationale | Multiple hedge layers create overlapping net exposure that is difficult to model and unwind cleanly. More than 2 layers is rarely justified. |\\n\\n## Rule 2: MaxHedgeExposure\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float (lot multiplier) |\\n| Default | BaseLot x 5.0 |\\n| Function | Maximum total lot size across all active hedge positions combined. If the computed hedge lot would exceed remaining capacity, the hedge lot is reduced to fit the remaining allowance. If no capacity remains, the request is blocked entirely. |\\n| Rationale | Prevents hedge positions from themselves becoming a source of excessive directional exposure that exceeds the protection they were designed to provide. |\\n\\n## Rule 3: MaxHedgeRatio\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float |\\n| Default | 1.0 (100% of current net original position exposure) |\\n| Recommended Range | 0.3 \\u2013 1.0 |\\n| Function | No hedge position may exceed this fraction of the current net original exposure at the time of placement. Formula: MaxHedgeLot = MaxHedgeRatio x NetExposureLots |\\n| On Violation | Hedge lot is capped at MaxHedgeLot. Order proceeds at the reduced lot size. |\\n| Rationale | Prevents over-hedging \\u2014 the condition where the hedge itself creates more directional exposure in the opposite direction than the original position. An over-hedged account has simply reversed its risk, not reduced it. |\\n\\n## Rule 4: HedgeCooldown\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (seconds) |\\n| Default | 600 seconds |\\n| Recommended Range | 300 \\u2013 3600 seconds |\\n| Function | Minimum elapsed time between consecutive hedge orders, applied per symbol. Measured from the timestamp of the previous hedge order fill on that symbol. |\\n| On Violation | Hedge request is queued. Re-evaluated after cooldown window expires. |\\n| Rationale | Prevents stacking hedge layers in rapid succession during high-volatility events such as news releases, where each additional layer compounds spread cost and complexity. |\\n\\n## Rule 5: HedgeTimeout\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Integer (seconds) |\\n| Default | 14400 seconds (4 hours) |\\n| Recommended Range | 3600 \\u2013 86400 seconds |\\n| Function | A background monitor checks all open hedge positions every minute. If any hedge position has been open longer than HedgeTimeout_Seconds without being unwound by normal exit conditions, it is automatically closed at market. |\\n| On Trigger | Forced market close. Event logged as HEDGE_TIMEOUT_UNWIND with the position ticket and elapsed time. Exit Engine executes the close. |\\n| Rationale | Hedges are temporary risk management instruments \\u2014 they are not intended as permanent positions. An open hedge accumulates swap cost indefinitely and locks capital that could be deployed more productively. The timeout forces resolution. |\\n\\n## Rule 6: EmergencyFlatten\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Float (percentage of account balance) |\\n| Default | 30.0% |\\n| Function | If the combined floating drawdown from ALL positions \\u2014 original, recovery, and active hedges \\u2014 reaches this threshold simultaneously, every open position is closed at market immediately. |\\n| On Trigger | Emergency market close all. EA transitions to HALTED state. No new orders of any type permitted until operator performs a manual restart with full review. |\\n| Rationale | Ensures that a failed hedge strategy does not compound the original drawdown beyond account survival. The hedge itself can become a liability if the market moves strongly in an unexpected direction \\u2014 this rule closes everything without exception. |\\n\\n## Rule 7: NoRecursiveHedge\\n\\n| Property | Detail |\\n|---|---|\\n| Type | Boolean |\\n| Value | true \\u2014 permanently enforced. This parameter is not exposed to configuration or optimization. |\\n| Function | Every hedge position is tagged with [HEDGE] in its comment field at placement. Before any hedge order is submitted, the engine checks whether the target net exposure includes positions already tagged [HEDGE]. If so, the request is blocked unconditionally. |\\n| On Violation | Blocked with error code HDG_004: RECURSIVE_HEDGE_BLOCKED. Logged with the ticket number of the [HEDGE]-tagged position that triggered the block. |\\n| Rationale | Recursive hedging creates a circular exposure structure that grows without bound on each iteration. It cannot be unwound without realizing a defined loss on every layer. This rule is non-negotiable and cannot be disabled. |\\n\\n## Default Configuration Summary\\n\\n```yaml\\nhedge_safety:\\n  MaxHedgeLayers: 2\\n  MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 5.0\\n  MaxHedgeRatio: 1.0\\n  HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600\\n  HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 14400\\n  EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 30.0\\n  NoRecursiveHedge: true   # Enforced. Not configurable. Not optimizable.\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 8 \\u2014 Authority Model\\n\\n## Authority Hierarchy\\n\\n**TIER 1 \\u2014 RISK ENGINE (Highest Authority)**\\n\\n\\\&quot;Account survival is the only non-negotiable rule.\\\&quot;\\n\\n- Monitors account equity and floating DD at every tick\\n- Sets and enforces CloseAllAtDD_Pct and EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct\\n- May override and terminate any engine action at any time with no preconditions\\n- Emits events: DD_THRESHOLD_BREACH, EMERGENCY_FLATTEN\\n- No engine may override or delay a Risk Engine directive\\n\\n**TIER 2 \\u2014 HEDGE ENGINE**\\n\\n\\\&quot;Reduce exposure before it becomes a crisis.\\\&quot;\\n\\n- Monitors net directional exposure and DD relative to hedge thresholds\\n- May suspend the Recovery Engine by emitting HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE\\n- May reduce the Signal Engine&#x27;s permission to open new trades\\n- Cannot override a Risk Engine directive\\n- Emits events: HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE, HEDGE_RESOLVED\\n\\n**TIER 3 \\u2014 RECOVERY ENGINE**\\n\\n\\\&quot;Improve position to escape loss efficiently.\\\&quot;\\n\\n- Monitors open position drawdown and trigger conditions\\n- May queue or block new Signal Engine entries during an active chain\\n- Cannot override a Hedge Engine or Risk Engine directive\\n- Suspended immediately upon receiving HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE\\n- Emits events: RECOVERY_CHAIN_ACTIVE, RECOVERY_CHAIN_CLOSED, RECOVERY_BREAK_EVEN_HIT\\n\\n**TIER 4 \\u2014 SIGNAL ENGINE (Lowest Authority)**\\n\\n\\\&quot;Generate directional trade opportunities.\\\&quot;\\n\\n- Monitors market conditions for new entry signals\\n- New trade entries blocked when RECOVERY_CHAIN_ACTIVE is active\\n- New trade entries blocked when HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE is active\\n- Provides trigger context and trend data upward to Recovery and Hedge Engines\\n- Cannot initiate any action that overrides a higher-tier engine state\\n\\n## Authority Rationale\\n\\n| Rule | Reasoning |\\n|---|---|\\n| Risk Engine ranks first | No strategy objective justifies account destruction. A signal may be wrong at any moment \\u2014 risk limits are structurally always correct. The Risk Engine must be able to act without waiting for any other engine&#x27;s consent. |\\n| Hedge ranks above Recovery | Recovery adds exposure. If the Hedge Engine has determined that exposure must be reduced, placing additional recovery orders directly contradicts that objective and must be prevented. |\\n| Recovery ranks above Signal | An active recovery chain changes the account&#x27;s position structure and break-even calculation. New signals entering while a recovery chain is active create orphaned positions that interfere with basket management and may prevent the chain from reaching break-even. |\\n| Signal ranks last | The Signal Engine generates opportunities. All other engines exist to manage the consequences of acting on those opportunities. Signals must always respect the current account state before being permitted to add new exposure. |\\n\\n## Cross-Engine Communication Protocol\\n\\n| Event | Emitter | Receiver | Action |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| DD_THRESHOLD_BREACH | Risk Engine | All Engines | Each engine evaluates its own suspension conditions |\\n| EMERGENCY_FLATTEN | Risk Engine | All Engines | All positions closed at market. All queues cleared. EA halts. |\\n| HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE | Hedge Engine | Recovery Engine | Recovery suspended. Log state change. |\\n| HEDGE_RESOLVED | Hedge Engine | Recovery Engine | Recovery engine may resume trigger evaluation |\\n| RECOVERY_CHAIN_ACTIVE | Recovery Engine | Signal Engine | New trade entries blocked. Queue any pending signals. |\\n| RECOVERY_CHAIN_CLOSED | Recovery Engine | Signal Engine | New trade entries permitted. Process queued signals. |\\n| RECOVERY_BREAK_EVEN_HIT | Recovery Engine | Exit Engine | Payload: {chain_id, tickets[], be_price, timestamp}. Close basket at market. |\\n| BASKET_BE_UPDATED | Basket Engine | Recovery Engine, Exit Engine | Payload: {chain_id, new_be_price, step_count, total_lots}. Recalculate exit targets. |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 9 \\u2014 Feature Gating\\n\\n## Design Principle\\n\\nFeature gating ensures that disabled modules have zero runtime cost, zero side effects, zero parameter surface, and are completely invisible to the optimizer.\\n\\n&gt; An optimizer running with RecoveryEnabled=false must not consider any recovery parameter in its optimization pass. Those parameters must not appear in the optimizer&#x27;s parameter space at all \\u2014 as if the recovery module does not exist in the EA.\\n\\n## Gate Registry\\n\\n| Gate Parameter | Type | Default | Effect When False or OFF |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| RecoveryEnabled | Boolean | false | All recovery parameters, triggers, order logic, and safety rules are disabled. Recovery module does not execute on any tick. |\\n| HedgeEnabled | Boolean | false | All hedge parameters, triggers, order logic, and safety rules are disabled. Hedge module does not execute on any tick. |\\n| RecoveryMode | Enum | NONE | When set to NONE, functionally identical to RecoveryEnabled=false. Generates a startup warning if RecoveryEnabled=true. |\\n| HedgeMode | Enum | OFF | When set to OFF, functionally identical to HedgeEnabled=false. Generates a startup warning if HedgeEnabled=true. |\\n| BasketExitEnabled | Boolean | true | When false, positions are managed individually with independent TP/SL. No basket break-even calculation is performed. |\\n| EmergencyFlattenEnabled | Boolean | true | Do not set to false in any production environment. This is the account&#x27;s last safety backstop. |\\n\\n## Optimizer Exclusion Rules\\n\\nWhen RecoveryEnabled = false, the following parameters must be excluded from the optimization parameter space:\\n- MaxRecoverySteps, RecoveryMultiplier, RecoveryDistance_Min_Points, RecoveryCooldown_Seconds, OneRecoveryChainAtATime, BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count, BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds, StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct, and all recovery trigger parameters\\n\\nWhen HedgeEnabled = false, the following parameters must be excluded:\\n- MaxHedgeLayers, MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier, MaxHedgeRatio, HedgeCooldown_Seconds, HedgeTimeout_Seconds, EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct, PartialHedgeDD_Pct, and all hedge trigger parameters\\n\\n## Gate Validation at EA Startup\\n\\nThe following checks run sequentially during OnInit(). Failure at any step halts the EA.\\n\\n**HALT definition:** The EA&#x27;s OnInit() function returns INIT_FAILED (-1), preventing the EA from attaching and trading. A log message with the relevant error code is written to the journal. No orders are placed. The operator must correct the configuration and restart.\\n\\n1. Read RecoveryEnabled. If true: initialize Recovery module. If false: skip all recovery initialization.\\n2. Read HedgeEnabled. If true: initialize Hedge module. If false: skip all hedge initialization.\\n3. If RecoveryEnabled is true AND RecoveryMode equals NONE: log WARNING REC_W001 \\u2014 \\\&quot;RecoveryEnabled=true but RecoveryMode=NONE \\u2014 no recovery will execute. This may be intentional during initial deployment.\\\&quot;\\n4. If HedgeEnabled is true AND HedgeMode equals OFF: log WARNING HDG_W001 \\u2014 \\\&quot;HedgeEnabled=true but HedgeMode=OFF \\u2014 no hedge will execute.\\\&quot;\\n5. If HedgeEnabled is true AND broker account type is NETTING (not HEDGING): log ERROR CFG_003 \\u2014 HALT. Hedging is not supported on netting accounts.\\n6. If CloseAllAtDD_Pct &lt;= StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: log ERROR CFG_001 \\u2014 \\\&quot;CloseAllAtDD_Pct must be strictly greater than StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct.\\\&quot; HALT.\\n7. If EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct &lt;= EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct: log WARNING CFG_W002 \\u2014 \\\&quot;EmergencyFlatten threshold is at or below EmergencyHedge threshold. EmergencyFlatten may trigger before the hedge has time to unwind. Review thresholds.\\\&quot;\\n8. If all checks pass: log INFO INIT_OK \\u2014 \\\&quot;Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine initialized successfully. RecoveryMode=[X] HedgeMode=[Y].\\\&quot;\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 10 \\u2014 Configuration Templates\\n\\n## Template 1: Trend Following EA\\n\\n```yaml\\n# EA Profile: Trend Following (EMA Crossover, MACD Trend, ADX Trend)\\n# Philosophy: Follow trend; recover on retracement with trend confirmation\\n\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: true\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE\\n  TriggerLogic: AND\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: DISTANCE\\n      method: ATR_MULTIPLIER\\n      atr_period: 14\\n      atr_multiplier: 1.2\\n    - type: TREND\\n      method: EMA_ALIGNMENT\\n      ema_fast: 20\\n      ema_slow: 50\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 3\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 30\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 12.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 22.0\\n\\nhedge:\\n  HedgeMode: PARTIAL\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: TREND_REVERSAL\\n      conditions: [EMA_CROSS, HALFTREND_FLIP]\\n      require_all: false\\n    - type: EMERGENCY_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 15.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxHedgeLayers: 2\\n    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.7\\n    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 4.0\\n    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600\\n    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 14400\\n    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 28.0\\n```\\n\\n## Template 2: HalfTrend EA\\n\\n```yaml\\n# EA Profile: HalfTrend EA\\n# Philosophy: Strong trend bias \\u2014 recover ONLY when HalfTrend signal is intact\\n\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: true\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE\\n  TriggerLogic: OR\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: TREND\\n      method: HALFTREND_VALID\\n    - type: CANDLE\\n      method: PIN_BAR\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 2\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 25\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 600\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 7200\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 12.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 22.0\\n\\nhedge:\\n  HedgeMode: TREND\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: TREND_REVERSAL\\n      conditions: [HALFTREND_FLIP]\\n    - type: EMERGENCY_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 15.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxHedgeLayers: 1\\n    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.8\\n    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 3.0\\n    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 900\\n    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 28800\\n    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 28.0\\n```\\n\\n## Template 3: SMC EA\\n\\n```yaml\\n# EA Profile: SMC (Smart Money Concepts) EA\\n# Philosophy: Structure-based entries; recover at key structure levels only\\n\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: true\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: ADAPTIVE\\n  TriggerLogic: AND\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: STRUCTURE\\n      method: HIGHER_LOW       # For BUY recovery\\n      # method: LOWER_HIGH    # For SELL recovery \\u2014 configure per direction\\n    - type: DISTANCE\\n      method: ATR_MULTIPLIER\\n      atr_period: 14\\n      atr_multiplier: 1.5\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 2\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 40\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 900\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 2\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 7200\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 10.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 20.0\\n\\nhedge:\\n  HedgeMode: TREND\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: STRUCTURE\\n      method: CHOCH_CONFIRMED\\n    - type: EMERGENCY_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 12.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxHedgeLayers: 2\\n    MaxHedgeRatio: 1.0\\n    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 4.0\\n    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 600\\n    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 21600\\n    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 25.0\\n```\\n\\n## Template 4: Grid EA\\n\\n```yaml\\n# EA Profile: Grid EA\\n# Philosophy: Grid IS a structured recovery system by design.\\n#             Hedge is emergency-only \\u2014 not a routine tool.\\n# NOTE: RecoveryCooldown_Seconds = 0 is intentional for Grid mode.\\n#       If RecoveryMode is ever changed to SOFT or ADAPTIVE for this EA,\\n#       RecoveryCooldown_Seconds must be set to a minimum of 60 to prevent\\n#       undefined order stacking behavior during rapid grid fills.\\n\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: true\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: CONTROLLED\\n  TriggerLogic: OR\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: DISTANCE\\n      method: FIXED_POINTS\\n      fixed_points: 50\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 5\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.0\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 50\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 0   # Grid fills are immediate by design\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: false\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 3\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 20.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 35.0\\n\\nhedge:\\n  HedgeMode: EMERGENCY\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: EMERGENCY_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 20.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxHedgeLayers: 1\\n    MaxHedgeRatio: 0.5\\n    MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier: 5.0\\n    HedgeCooldown_Seconds: 1800\\n    HedgeTimeout_Seconds: 43200\\n    EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct: 40.0\\n```\\n\\n## Template 5: Mean Reversion EA\\n\\n```yaml\\n# EA Profile: Mean Reversion EA (Bollinger Band bounce, RSI extreme)\\n# Philosophy: Recover during over-extension; hedge rarely needed or wanted\\n\\nfeature_gates:\\n  RecoveryEnabled: true\\n  HedgeEnabled: false     # Mean reversion assumes range \\u2014 hedge conflicts with this\\n  BasketExitEnabled: true\\n  # All hedge parameters below are ignored when HedgeEnabled=false\\n\\nrecovery:\\n  RecoveryMode: CONTROLLED\\n  TriggerLogic: OR\\n  Triggers:\\n    - type: VOLATILITY\\n      method: ATR_CONTRACTION\\n    - type: DD\\n      method: FLOATING_DD\\n      threshold_pct: 5.0\\n  safety:\\n    MaxRecoverySteps: 3\\n    RecoveryMultiplier: 1.5\\n    RecoveryDistance_Min_Points: 20\\n    RecoveryCooldown_Seconds: 300\\n    OneRecoveryChainAtATime: true\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count: 3\\n    BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds: 3600\\n    StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct: 10.0\\n    CloseAllAtDD_Pct: 18.0\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# SECTION 11 \\u2014 EA Platform Integration\\n\\n## Full System Integration Map\\n\\n```\\n[SIGNAL ENGINE]\\n     | SIGNAL_GENERATED\\n     | TREND_SCORE_UPDATE\\n     v\\n[ENTRY ENGINE] &lt;-------- BLOCK_NEW_SIGNALS (from Recovery/Hedge)\\n     | POSITION_OPENED\\n     v\\n[RECOVERY &amp; HEDGE ENGINE]\\n  |\\n  |-- [RECOVERY MODULE]          [HEDGE MODULE]\\n  |   Trigger eval                Trigger eval\\n  |   Safety gauntlet             Safety gauntlet\\n  |   Order generation            Order generation\\n  |   Basket management           Hedge unwind monitor\\n  |          |                          |\\n  |   RECOVERY_ORDER_REQUEST      HEDGE_ORDER_REQUEST\\n  |          |                          |\\n  `----------+--------------------------+----------&gt; [ENTRY ENGINE]\\n             |                          |\\n  BASKET_EXIT_REQUEST          HEDGE_UNWIND_REQUEST\\n             |                          |\\n             v                          v\\n         [EXIT ENGINE]\\n           |-- Basket break-even exits (Recovery)\\n           |-- Individual TP/SL exits (Signal)\\n           |-- Hedge unwind exits (Hedge)\\n           `-- Emergency flatten (Risk Engine command)\\n             |\\n             v\\n         [RISK ENGINE]\\n           |-- Account DD monitoring every tick\\n           |-- CloseAllAtDD override \\u2014 no consent required\\n           `-- Emits: DD_THRESHOLD_BREACH, EMERGENCY_FLATTEN\\n             |\\n             v\\n         [BASKET ENGINE]\\n           |-- Tracks all positions by chain_id\\n           |-- Calculates weighted average entry (break-even)\\n           |-- Tags: ORIGINAL, RECOVERY, HEDGE\\n           |-- Emits BASKET_BE_UPDATED on every new recovery step\\n           `-- Provides NetExposureLots for hedge ratio calculations\\n             |\\n             v\\n     [DIAGNOSTICS &amp; LOGGING]\\n       All engine events: [TIMESTAMP][ENGINE][EVENT][SYMBOL][DETAIL]\\n             |\\n             v\\n     [PORTFOLIO GUARDIAN]\\n       Inputs:  All open positions, correlation matrix, balance/equity/margin\\n       Outputs to Risk Engine:  PortfolioDD_Pct, CorrelatedExposure_Pct\\n       Outputs to Hedge Engine: PORTFOLIO_HEDGE_SIGNAL\\n       Authority: Observer and reporter only. Does not place or modify orders.\\n```\\n\\n## Integration Contracts\\n\\n### Signal Engine to Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine\\n\\n| Event | Payload |\\n|---|---|\\n| SIGNAL_GENERATED | symbol, direction, strength, trigger_type |\\n| SIGNAL_INVALIDATED | symbol, reason \\u2014 may trigger Trend Recovery re-evaluation |\\n| TREND_SCORE_UPDATE | symbol, score (0\\u2013100), direction \\u2014 consumed by Trend Recovery trigger |\\n\\n### Entry Engine to Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine\\n\\n| Event | Payload |\\n|---|---|\\n| POSITION_OPENED | ticket, symbol, direction, lot, entry_price, chain_id, tag (ORIGINAL / RECOVERY / HEDGE) |\\n| ENTRY_BLOCKED | reason \\u2014 R&amp;H state caused block \\u2014 logged for diagnostics |\\n\\n### Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine to Entry Engine\\n\\n| Event | Payload |\\n|---|---|\\n| BLOCK_NEW_SIGNALS | reason: RECOVERY_CHAIN_ACTIVE or HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE |\\n| RECOVERY_ORDER_REQUEST | symbol, direction, lot, type (MARKET/PENDING), chain_id, step_number |\\n| HEDGE_ORDER_REQUEST | symbol, direction, lot, mode, layer_number, hedge_id, parent_chain_id |\\n\\n### Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine to Exit Engine\\n\\n| Event | Payload |\\n|---|---|\\n| BASKET_EXIT_REQUEST | chain_id, tickets[], be_price, reason: BREAK_EVEN_HIT |\\n| HEDGE_UNWIND_REQUEST | hedge_id, tickets[], reason: CONDITION_CLEARED or TIMEOUT |\\n| RECOVERY_MODE_HEDGE_UNWIND | hedge_id, chain_id, reason: RECOVERY_CHAIN_CLOSED \\u2014 simultaneous unwind required |\\n| EMERGENCY_FLATTEN | source: RISK_ENGINE, dd_pct, timestamp \\u2014 closes all |\\n\\n### Basket Engine to Recovery &amp; Hedge Engine\\n\\n| Data | Trigger | Payload |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| BASKET_BE_UPDATED | Every new recovery step fill | chain_id, new_be_price, step_count, total_lots, floating_pnl |\\n| NetExposureLots | On every position open/close | symbol, net_lots, direction |\\n| BasketFloatingPL | Every tick | chain_id, floating_pnl, dd_pct |\\n| ChainPositions[] | On request | tickets[], lots[], entry_prices[], tags[] |\\n\\n## Logging Standard\\n\\nEvery engine action is logged in this standardized format:\\n\\n`[TIMESTAMP]  [ENGINE]   [EVENT_TYPE]       [SYMBOL]  [DETAIL]`\\n\\nExamples:\\n\\n- 2026.06.06 14:00:01  RECOVERY  STEP_PLACED        EURUSD  Step=2, Lot=0.023, Entry=1.08420, Chain=C001, BE=1.08510\\n- 2026.06.06 14:00:01  BASKET    BE_UPDATED         EURUSD  Chain=C001, NewBE=1.08510, Steps=2, TotalLots=0.048\\n- 2026.06.06 14:05:22  SAFETY    REC_001_BLOCKED    EURUSD  Rule=MaxRecoverySteps, Current=3, Limit=3\\n- 2026.06.06 14:15:44  HEDGE     EMERGENCY_PLACED   EURUSD  Mode=EMERGENCY, Lot=0.10, DD=15.3%, HedgeID=H001\\n- 2026.06.06 14:22:11  RISK      FLATTEN_ALL        ALL     DD=25.2%, Threshold=25.0%, Trigger=CloseAllAtDD\\n- 2026.06.06 14:22:12  EXIT      FLATTEN_EXEC       EURUSD  Tickets=[1001,1002,1003], Lots=[0.01,0.015,0.023], Reason=EMERGENCY\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Appendix A \\u2014 Parameter Quick Reference\\n\\n## Recovery Parameters\\n\\n| Parameter | Type | Default | Range | Description |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| RecoveryEnabled | Bool | false | \\u2014 | Master gate \\u2014 disables entire module |\\n| RecoveryMode | Enum | NONE | NONE / SOFT / ADAPTIVE / CONTROLLED | Operating mode |\\n| MaxRecoverySteps | Int | 3 | 1 \\u2013 5 | Max recovery orders per chain |\\n| RecoveryMultiplier | Float | 1.5 | 1.0 \\u2013 2.0 | Lot size multiplier per step |\\n| RecoveryDistance_Min_Points | Int | 20 | 10 \\u2013 100 | Min separation between entries (pts) |\\n| RecoveryCooldown_Seconds | Int | 300 | 60 \\u2013 1800 | Min time between recovery steps |\\n| OneRecoveryChainAtATime | Bool | true | \\u2014 | Portfolio-level chain limit |\\n| BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Count | Int | 2 | 1 \\u2013 5 | Consecutive loss count to trigger block |\\n| BlockRecoveryAfterLoss_Cooldown_Seconds | Int | 3600 | 1800 \\u2013 86400 | Duration of recovery block after loss |\\n| StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct | Float | 15.0 | 5 \\u2013 25 | Floating DD % that suspends recovery |\\n| CloseAllAtDD_Pct | Float | 25.0 | 15 \\u2013 40 | Floating DD % that closes ALL positions |\\n\\n## Hedge Parameters\\n\\n| Parameter | Type | Default | Range | Description |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| HedgeEnabled | Bool | false | \\u2014 | Master gate \\u2014 disables entire module |\\n| HedgeMode | Enum | OFF | OFF / EMERGENCY / PARTIAL / BREAKOUT / TREND / RECOVERY | Operating mode |\\n| MaxHedgeLayers | Int | 2 | 1 \\u2013 3 | Max simultaneous hedge positions |\\n| MaxHedgeExposure_LotMultiplier | Float | 5.0 | 1.0 \\u2013 10.0 | Max hedge lots = BaseLot x multiplier |\\n| MaxHedgeRatio | Float | 1.0 | 0.3 \\u2013 1.0 | Max hedge as fraction of net exposure |\\n| HedgeCooldown_Seconds | Int | 600 | 300 \\u2013 3600 | Min time between hedge orders |\\n| HedgeTimeout_Seconds | Int | 14400 | 3600 \\u2013 86400 | Auto-unwind timeout |\\n| EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct | Float | 15.0 | 10 \\u2013 30 | DD % that triggers EMERGENCY hedge |\\n| PartialHedgeDD_Pct | Float | 8.0 | 5 \\u2013 20 | DD % that triggers PARTIAL hedge |\\n| EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct | Float | 30.0 | 20 \\u2013 50 | Combined DD % that closes ALL positions |\\n| NoRecursiveHedge | Bool | true | enforced | Cannot hedge a position already tagged HEDGE |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Appendix B \\u2014 Error Codes\\n\\n| Code | Engine | Meaning | Correct Action |\\n|---|---|---|---|\\n| REC_001 | Recovery | MaxRecoverySteps ceiling reached | Hold position. Log. Basket managed by Exit Engine only. |\\n| REC_002 | Recovery | Computed lot would exceed MaxLotSize | Block the recovery order entirely. No partial lot reduction. Log. |\\n| REC_003 | Recovery | RecoveryCooldown window still active | Queue request. Re-evaluate after cooldown expires. |\\n| REC_004 | Recovery | OneRecoveryChainAtATime \\u2014 another chain already active | Block. Log. New chain permitted only after active chain resolves. |\\n| REC_005 | Recovery | BlockRecoveryAfterLoss cooling state active | Block all recovery. Log. Resume automatically after cooldown expires. |\\n| REC_006 | Recovery | StopRecoveryAtDD threshold breached | Suspend all recovery. Basket held. Exit Engine takes control. |\\n| REC_007 | Recovery | Hedge Engine emitted HEDGE_EMERGENCY_ACTIVE | Suspend recovery. Log. Resume on HEDGE_RESOLVED event. |\\n| HDG_001 | Hedge | MaxHedgeLayers ceiling reached | Block new hedge. Log. Existing hedges continue. |\\n| HDG_002 | Hedge | Computed lot exceeds MaxHedgeRatio cap | Reduce hedge lot to ratio cap and proceed. Log reduction. |\\n| HDG_003 | Hedge | HedgeCooldown window still active | Queue request. Re-evaluate after cooldown expires. |\\n| HDG_004 | Hedge | NoRecursiveHedge \\u2014 target position is tagged [HEDGE] | Block unconditionally. Log with target ticket number. |\\n| HDG_005 | Hedge | HedgeTimeout elapsed without normal unwind | Force market close of hedge position. Log as HEDGE_TIMEOUT_UNWIND. |\\n| HDG_006 | Hedge | Broker account is NETTING mode \\u2014 hedging not supported | Startup halt. Return INIT_FAILED. |\\n| RISK_001 | Risk | CloseAllAtDD threshold breached | Emergency flatten all positions. EA enters HALTED state. |\\n| RISK_002 | Risk | EmergencyFlatten threshold breached (combined positions) | Emergency flatten all positions. EA enters HALTED state. |\\n| CFG_001 | Config | CloseAllAtDD_Pct &lt;= StopRecoveryAtDD_Pct | Config error. Startup halt. Return INIT_FAILED. |\\n| CFG_W002 | Config | EmergencyFlatten_DD_Pct &lt;= EmergencyHedgeDD_Pct | Warning only. Thresholds should be reviewed but EA can initialize. |\\n| CFG_003 | Config | HedgeEnabled=true but broker is NETTING mode | Startup halt. Return INIT_FAILED. Set HedgeEnabled=false. |\\n| REC_W001 | Config | RecoveryEnabled=true but RecoveryMode=NONE | Warning only. No recovery will execute. Likely intentional during initial deployment. |\\n| HDG_W001 | Config | HedgeEnabled=true but HedgeMode=OFF | Warning only. No hedge will execute. Likely intentional during staged activation. |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Appendix C \\u2014 Glossary\\n\\n| Term | Definition |\\n|---|---|\\n| Recovery Chain | A group of positions (original entry plus all recovery entries) managed together as a single coordinated basket |\\n| Chain ID | Unique identifier assigned to a recovery chain at the moment of original entry (e.g. C001, C002) |\\n| Break-Even (BE) | The weighted average entry price across all positions in a basket. Exiting the entire basket at this price produces zero profit/loss before spread. |\\n| BaseLot | The original lot size placed by the Signal Engine at the first trade entry |\\n| Recovery Step | Each additional recovery order placed after the original entry. Step 0 = original. Steps 1 through N = recovery entries. |\\n| Hedge Layer | One individual hedge position. Multiple layers means multiple separate hedge positions open simultaneously. |\\n| Hedge Ratio | HedgeLot / NetExposureLot. Represents the fraction of net directional exposure that has been neutralized by the hedge. |\\n| Net Exposure | Sum of all BUY lots minus sum of all SELL lots for a given symbol. Positive = net long. Negative = net short. |\\n| Feature Gate | A boolean or enum parameter that enables or disables an entire engine module with zero side effects when disabled. |\\n| Authority | The tier rank of an engine in the hierarchy. Higher authority engines may override lower authority engines without consent. |\\n| Recursive Hedge | A hedge placed on a position that is itself already a hedge. Prohibited unconditionally by NoRecursiveHedge. |\\n| Basket Exit | Closing all positions in a recovery chain simultaneously at market when the basket reaches its break-even target. |\\n| HALTED State | The EA operational state after an emergency flatten event. No orders of any type are permitted until a manual operator restart. |\\n| DD | Drawdown. Expressed as: (Floating Loss / Account Balance) x 100. Always a positive percentage value. |\\n| ATR | Average True Range. A volatility indicator measuring the average price range over a defined period. Used for dynamic distance calculations. |\\n| BOS | Break of Structure. SMC concept: price breaks a prior significant swing high or swing low, signaling a potential directional continuation. |\\n| CHoCH | Change of Character. SMC concept: the market structure shifts from bullish (Higher Highs, Higher Lows) to bearish (Lower Highs, Lower Lows) or vice versa. |\\n| HL | Higher Low. Bullish structure: each successive pullback terminates at a price level above the previous pullback&#x27;s low. |\\n| LH | Lower High. Bearish structure: each successive rally terminates at a price level below the previous rally&#x27;s high. |\\n| Logic Combiner | The engine component that evaluates multiple trigger conditions and applies AND, OR, or WEIGHTED logic to determine whether a recovery or hedge action fires. |\\n| BASKET_BE_UPDATED | Engine event emitted by the Basket Engine every time a new recovery step is filled, carrying the recalculated break-even price to all consuming engines. |\&quot;, \&quot;confidentiality_level\&quot;: \&quot;internal\&quot;}&quot;" result="&quot;\n\n---\n### ✅ Ready\n📁 **recoveryandhedgeengine_1782784702.docx**\n🔗 [Download](https://scgc-ailylab.scg.com/mcp/docsgenerate/files/ac3fa0a0169a176044e4932d5b18f4aa.docx?exp=1782820703&amp;sig=8f284ae43c8e0cdba3c91982652aa13e71833e0cf3f31b74b857c2cd5ada908f)\n---\n&quot;" files="" embeds="&quot;&quot;">

### สรุปสิ่งที่แก้ไขใน v1.1.0

| # | Issue | การแก้ไข |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | ไม่มี Mode Selection Decision Tree | เพิ่ม 4-step decision tree ใน Section 2 |
| 2 | ไม่มี Trigger Logic Combiner | เพิ่ม AND/OR/WEIGHTED combiner พร้อม ASCII diagram ใน Section 3 |
| 3 | CloseAllAtDD constraint ไม่ชัด | เพิ่ม hard constraint note ตรงใน Rule 8 + YAML comment |
| 4 | RECOVERY mode ไม่ระบุ exit authority | เพิ่ม Exit Authority clause — hedge ต้อง unwind พร้อม recovery chain |
| 5 | Breakout strength ไม่มี scoring | เพิ่ม Breakout Strength Score table (0–100 → ratio mapping) |
| 6 | `RECOVERY_BREAK_EVEN_HIT` payload ไม่ครบ | เพิ่ม `{chain_id, tickets[], be_price, timestamp}` |
| 7 | HALT ไม่มี definition | เพิ่ม HALT definition = `INIT_FAILED` / no trading until manual restart |
| 8 | Grid `RecoveryCooldown=0` ไม่มี guard | เพิ่ม YAML comment warning สำหรับ mode change |
| 9 | ไม่มี `BASKET_BE_UPDATED` feedback event | เพิ่มใน Section 8 + Section 11 contracts + Glossary |
| 10 | `REC_002` "reduce or block" inconsistent | แก้เป็น "block only" — ตรงกับ Rule 2 definition |
| 11 | ไม่มี Version Changelog | เพิ่ม Changelog table ที่หน้าแรก |
| 12 | ไม่มี ASCII flow diagrams | เพิ่ม flow diagram ใน Section 2, 4, 7, 11 |

> ลิงก์มีอายุ **10 ชั่วโมง** ครับ
